# Phone storyboard harness correction

Base: `71ef5dc5` (`codex/wt-fix-phone-20261011`). Working branch:
`codex/wt-phone-harness-20261011`.

The old `storyboardPhone` fixture created a standalone egg, then invoked pairing
administration without starting the main wing. Its `storyboardCommand` also
waited for `wt pair` to exit without supplying a phone or terminal approval.
Those are fixture failures: an egg is not the pairing authority, and pairing
is an interactive two-sided operation.

[The pairing protocol](docs/pairing/PROTOCOL.md), opening contract, explicitly
requires `wt pair` to contact the running main wing's local control endpoint.
“Invitation and transport” specifies the real-user sequence `wt wing start`
then `wt pair`, and accountless local operation. “PAKE and two-sided
confirmation” requires independent phone confirmation and terminal Enter.
“Administration and revocation” requires `wt devices` and revoke to contact
that same main wing.

## Fixture changes

- Start the actual built CLI with `wt wing start --paths <fixture workspace>`.
  Northstar already supplies a fresh isolated `HOME`, `WINGTHING_DIR`, PATH,
  temporary directory and disposable egg. No registration/account token is
  present, so this uses the ordinary accountless local startup path. Register
  `wt wing stop` cleanup before startup and run it before removing state.
- Use the existing Go `internal/phonepair/testpeer --client` as the phone.
  Drive the real PTY `wt pair --show-link` command, decode its real invitation,
  perform PAKE proof, verify the returned emoji, confirm on the phone, check
  that this alone did not issue a grant, then send terminal Enter. Wait for
  successful CLI exit and drain its actual output before returning it to the
  unchanged card assertions. `--show-link` is the existing explicit fallback
  route; it does not fabricate QR/camera evidence.
- Recover the encrypted grant, authenticate, inventory the one existing
  blocked session, attach/read its marker, and detach. Check that the main
  wing PID is unchanged. The peer keeps its signing key and credential only
  in process memory; the wing persists the actual protocol device record.
- Card 6 now has a genuinely paired device before listing. Card 11's existing
  prerequisite explicitly expects an unpaired-device error. Pair and revoke
  through the real CLI first, verify reconnect is rejected, then run the
  original revoke command against that revoked device. Both calls and the
  real success/error outputs are recorded; no response text is synthesized.
- Keep non-CGO builds compiling through a driver that retains their explicit
  unsupported-pairing behavior. The native Go testpeer path requires CGO as
  specified by the protocol.

The original card file has exactly two fixture edits: add main-wing startup
and call the interactive phone command driver. Every original assertion,
expected string, command selection, timeout, test name and final fatal check
is byte-identical. The other three storyboard card files are unchanged. The
existing CLI journey assertions are also unchanged; an additional harness
prerequisite test provides a separately reportable executable result.

## Acceptance boundary

All 18 original phone cards end with an unconditional
`t.Fatal("REQUIRED-UNRUN: ... physical iPhone evidence required")` even after
all prerequisites succeed. That statement remains unchanged. Therefore raw
card outcomes must remain FAIL, and the physical obligations must be reported
as REQUIRED-UNRUN. A successful Go peer cannot establish native phone UI,
camera scan, device Keychain/Secure Enclave, cellular reachability, permission
flows or background notifications. The protocol explicitly preserves this
boundary. Backend evidence cannot close a physical-card bead.

Results and gate comparison: [evidence/harness/RESULTS.md](evidence/harness/RESULTS.md).
Independent assertion review: [HARNESS-REVIEW.md](HARNESS-REVIEW.md).

@@ -109,80 +109,203 @@ func phoneLinkInvitation(t *testing.T, h *northstarPTY) phonepair.Invitation {
 	if start < 0 {
 		t.Fatal("CLI did not display requested fallback link")
 	}
 	packed := strings.Fields(out[start:])[0]
 	b, e := base64.RawURLEncoding.DecodeString(strings.TrimPrefix(packed, "wingthing://pair#v1z."))
 	if e != nil {
 		t.Fatal(e)
 	}
 	reader := flate.NewReader(bytes.NewReader(b))
 	defer reader.Close()
 	var parts []json.RawMessage
 	if e = json.NewDecoder(io.LimitReader(reader, 4096)).Decode(&parts); e != nil || len(parts) != 9 {
 		t.Fatal("invalid invitation", e)
 	}
 	var q phonepair.Invitation
 	for i, target := range []any{&q.Version, &q.WingID, &q.Name, &q.Key} {
 		if e = json.Unmarshal(parts[i], target); e != nil {
 			t.Fatal(e)
 		}
 	}
 	if e = json.Unmarshal(parts[4], &q.Local); e != nil {
 		var endpoints []string
 		if e = json.Unmarshal(parts[4], &endpoints); e != nil || len(endpoints) == 0 {
 			t.Fatal("invalid direct endpoints")
 		}
 		q.Local, q.Endpoints = endpoints[0], endpoints[1:]
 	}
 	for i, target := range []any{&q.Relay, &q.ID, &q.Secret} {
 		if e = json.Unmarshal(parts[i+5], target); e != nil {
 			t.Fatal(e)
 		}
 	}
 	var seconds int64
 	if e = json.Unmarshal(parts[8], &seconds); e != nil {
 		t.Fatal(e)
 	}
 	q.ExpiresAt = time.Unix(seconds, 0)
 	return q
 }
 
+// Drive the real interactive pair command to completion. storyboardCommand
+// alone waits for an exit, but pairing waits for a phone and terminal approval.
+// The testpeer is the phone; neither a registry entry nor a CLI screen is faked.
+func storyboardPhoneCommand(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture, binary string, args ...string) (string, error) {
+	t.Helper()
+	pid, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(f.local, "wing.pid"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	h := northstarStartPTY(t, f, binary, 160, 60, "pair", "--show-link")
+	h.expect("phone fixture invitation", 0, "Pair a phone", "5:00", "Waiting for scan", "wingthing://pair#v1z.")
+	q := phoneLinkInvitation(t, h)
+	peer := newStoryboardPeer(t, f)
+	peer.ok(t, map[string]any{"Op": "init", "Invitation": q, "Endpoint": q.Local})
+	peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "start"})
+	proof := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "prove", "Name": "Bryan's iPhone"})
+	emoji, ok := proof["emoji"].(string)
+	if !ok || emoji == "" {
+		t.Fatal("phone fixture did not receive transcript-bound emoji")
+	}
+	h.expect("phone fixture confirmation", 0, emoji, "Bryan's iPhone wants to pair")
+	pending := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "confirm"})
+	if pending["state"] != "confirming" || pending["device_id"] != nil {
+		t.Fatal("phone fixture gained access without terminal approval")
+	}
+	h.send("\r")
+	h.expect("phone fixture paired", 0, "Paired Bryan's iPhone")
+	var exitErr error
+	select {
+	case exitErr = <-h.done:
+	case <-time.After(20 * time.Second):
+		t.Fatal("phone fixture pair did not exit")
+	}
+	if exitErr != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("phone fixture pair failed: %v", exitErr)
+	}
+	if err := h.slave.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	select {
+	case <-h.readDone:
+	case <-time.After(3 * time.Second):
+		t.Fatal("phone fixture pair PTY did not drain")
+	}
+	h.mu.Lock()
+	out := h.transcript.String()
+	h.mu.Unlock()
+	grant := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "status"})
+	if grant["device_id"] == nil {
+		t.Fatal("phone fixture did not obtain a device grant")
+	}
+	peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "connect"})
+	inventory := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "sessions"})
+	rows, ok := inventory["result"].([]any)
+	if !ok || len(rows) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("phone fixture saw unexpected wing inventory: %v", inventory)
+	}
+	row, ok := rows[0].(map[string]any)
+	if !ok || row["id"] != "phone-session" || row["blocked"] != true {
+		t.Fatalf("phone fixture lost session identity/attention: %v", inventory)
+	}
+	peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "attach", "SessionID": "phone-session"})
+	read := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "read", "SessionID": "phone-session"})
+	result, ok := read["result"].(map[string]any)
+	screen, _ := result["screen"].(string)
+	if !ok || !strings.Contains(screen, "PHONE_SESSION_UNCHANGED") {
+		t.Fatalf("phone fixture read unexpected session: %v", read)
+	}
+	peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "detach"})
+	after, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(f.local, "wing.pid"))
+	if err != nil || !bytes.Equal(pid, after) {
+		t.Fatal("phone fixture pairing replaced the main wing")
+	}
+	if args[0] == "pair" {
+		return out, exitErr
+	}
+	if args[0] == "devices" && len(args) > 1 && args[1] == "revoke" {
+		// Card 11's unchanged prerequisite expects an unpaired-device error.
+		// Exercise a real grant and revocation first, then retain that state for
+		// the original command. No missing-device copy is fabricated.
+		revokedOut, err := storyboardCommand(t, f, binary, args...)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("phone fixture revoke: %v\n%s", err, revokedOut)
+		}
+		storyboardContains(t, revokedOut, "Revoked Bryan's iPhone", "Live access closed")
+		revoked := peer.call(t, map[string]string{"Op": "connect"})
+		if revoked["error"] != "revoked" {
+			t.Fatal("phone fixture revocation allowed reconnect", revoked)
+		}
+	}
+	return storyboardCommand(t, f, binary, args...)
+}
+
+// This checks the repaired prerequisites separately from the original cards'
+// unconditional physical-device fatal assertion. It does not replace a card.
+func TestPhoneStoryboardHarnessPrerequisites(t *testing.T) {
+	for _, command := range [][]string{{"pair"}, {"devices"}, {"devices", "revoke", "Bryan's iPhone"}} {
+		t.Run(strings.Join(command, "-"), func(t *testing.T) {
+			f, binary := storyboardFixture(t)
+			northstarStandaloneEgg(t, f, f.local, "phone-session", "pairing", "needs_input", "PHONE_SESSION_UNCHANGED")
+			storyboardPhoneMainWing(t, f, binary)
+			out, err := storyboardPhoneCommand(t, f, binary, command...)
+			switch {
+			case command[0] == "pair":
+				if err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				storyboardContains(t, out, "Pair a phone", "5:00", "Waiting for scan")
+			case len(command) == 1:
+				if err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				storyboardContains(t, out, "NAME", "PAIRED AT", "LAST SEEN", "Bryan's iPhone")
+			default:
+				if err == nil {
+					t.Fatal("unpaired revoke unexpectedly succeeded")
+				}
+				storyboardContains(t, out, "device", "not paired")
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}

 func TestPhoneStoryboardWingJourney(t *testing.T) {
 	f, binary := storyboardFixture(t)
 	northstarWrite(t, filepath.Join(f.bin, "tailscale"), `#!/bin/sh
 [ "$1 $2" = "status --json" ] || exit 1
 printf '%s\n' '{"BackendState":"Running","Self":{"TailscaleIPs":["100.91.2.3"],"DNSName":"EhrlichMac.fixture.ts.net."}}'
 `, 0700)
 	local := northstarStandaloneEgg(t, f, f.local, "phone-session", "pairing", "needs_input", "PHONE_SESSION_UNCHANGED")
 	remote := northstarStandaloneEgg(t, f, f.remote, "phone-session", "other-wing", "working", "OTHER_WING_UNCHANGED")
 	out, e := storyboardCommand(t, f, binary, "wing", "start", "--paths", f.work)
 	if e != nil {
 		t.Fatalf("main wing startup: %v\n%s", e, out)
 	}
 	storyboardContains(t, out, "local control: ready", "Local network only. That's fine; nothing else needed.")
 	t.Cleanup(func() {
 		cmd := exec.Command(binary, "wing", "stop")
 		cmd.Env = f.env
 		cmd.Dir = f.work
 		if out, e := cmd.CombinedOutput(); e != nil {
 			t.Errorf("stop isolated wing: %v\n%s", e, out)
 		}
 	})
 	pid, e := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(f.local, "wing.pid"))
 	if e != nil {
 		t.Fatal(e)
 	}
 	var control phonepair.Control
 	b, e := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(f.local, "phone-control.json"))
 	if e != nil {
 		t.Fatal(e)
 	}
 	if e = json.Unmarshal(b, &control); e != nil {
 		t.Fatal(e)
 	}
 	h := northstarStartPTY(t, f, binary, 160, 60, "pair", "--show-link")
 	h.expect("live invitation", 0, "Pair a phone", "5:00", "Waiting for scan", "wingthing://pair#v1z.")
 	q := phoneLinkInvitation(t, h)
 	if q.WingID != control.WingID || q.Key != control.Key || len(strings.Fields(q.Secret)) != 6 || q.Relay != "" || time.Until(q.ExpiresAt) > 5*time.Minute {
 		t.Fatal("invitation lost main-wing identity/lifetime/local-only binding")

 func TestStoryboardMachinesCard4(t *testing.T) {

 			out, err := storyboardCommand(t, f, binary, "attach", "forge:same")
 			if err == nil {
 				t.Fatal("unverified route allowed")
 			}
 			storyboardContains(t, out, reason, "session outcome unknown")

 				t.Fatal("identity/version failure crossed route")
 			}
 		})
 	}
 }
 
 // Phone-only assertions have an executable prerequisite. Stop at its observed
 // failure, rather than fabricate downstream iOS screens/crypto receipts on Linux.
 // The map spells out each unexercised downstream screen and state obligation.
 func storyboardPhone(t *testing.T, card int) {
 	f, binary := storyboardFixture(t)
 	northstarStandaloneEgg(t, f, f.local, "phone-session", "pairing", "needs_input", "PHONE_SESSION_UNCHANGED")
+	storyboardPhoneMainWing(t, f, binary)
 	command := []string{"pair"}
 	prerequisite := []string{"Pair a phone", "5:00", "Waiting for scan"}
 	if card == 6 {
 		command = []string{"devices"}
 		prerequisite = []string{"NAME", "PAIRED AT", "LAST SEEN"}
 	}
 	if card == 11 {
 		command = []string{"devices", "revoke", "Bryan's iPhone"}
 		prerequisite = []string{"device", "not paired"}
 	}
-	out, err := storyboardCommand(t, f, binary, command...)
+	out, err := storyboardPhoneCommand(t, f, binary, command...)
 	entries, _ := os.ReadDir(filepath.Join(f.remote, "eggs"))
 	if len(entries) != 0 {
 		t.Fatal("phone prerequisite admitted work on another wing")
 	}
 	t.Logf("card 05/#%d prerequisite: %v; downstream physical-device assertions are NOT exercised", card+1, command)
 	if err != nil && strings.Contains(out, "unknown command") {
 		t.Fatalf("phone card blocked: required wt %s unavailable; expected prerequisite screen %q; actual:\n%s", command[0], prerequisite, out)
 	}
 	storyboardContains(t, out, prerequisite...)
 	t.Fatal("REQUIRED-UNRUN: invitation prerequisite alone cannot prove this card's phone screen, device Keychain, encrypted RPC, route or revocation assertions; physical iPhone evidence required")
 }

     2	
     3	import (
     4		"os/exec"
     5		"testing"
     6	)
     7	
     8	// Pairing administration belongs to the existing main wing, not an egg.
     9	// Start it through the public CLI, using Northstar's isolated HOME and state.
    10	func storyboardPhoneMainWing(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture, binary string) {
    11		t.Helper()
    12		// Register cleanup before startup so even a partial readiness failure is
    13		// cleaned up while the fixture's state directory and egg are still alive.
    14		t.Cleanup(func() {
    15			cmd := exec.Command(binary, "wing", "stop")
    16			cmd.Env, cmd.Dir = f.env, f.work
    17			if out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
    18				t.Errorf("stop isolated main wing: %v\n%s", err, out)
    19			}
    20		})
    21		out, err := storyboardCommand(t, f, binary, "wing", "start", "--paths", f.work)
    22		if err != nil {
    23			t.Fatalf("start isolated main wing: %v\n%s", err, out)
    24		}
    25		storyboardContains(t, out, "local control: ready", "Local network only. That's fine; nothing else needed.")
    26	}

    11		return storyboardCommand(t, f, binary, args...)

    22	type storyboardPeer struct {
    23		in  io.WriteCloser
    24		out *bufio.Reader
    25	}
    26	
    27	func newStoryboardPeer(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture) *storyboardPeer {
    28		t.Helper()
    29		binary := filepath.Join(f.root, "testpeer")
    30		build := exec.CommandContext(t.Context(), "nice", "-n", "15", "go", "build", "-buildvcs=false", "-o", binary, "./internal/phonepair/testpeer")
    31		build.Dir = f.repo
    32		build.Env = append(os.Environ(), "GOFLAGS=-p=4", "GOCACHE="+filepath.Join(f.repo, ".scratch/northstar/go-build"), "GOMODCACHE="+filepath.Join(f.repo, ".scratch/northstar/go-mod"))
    33		if out, e := build.CombinedOutput(); e != nil {
    34			t.Fatalf("testpeer build: %v\n%s", e, out)
    35		}
    36		cmd := exec.Command(binary, "--client")
    37		cmd.Env = f.env
    38		cmd.Dir = f.work
    39		in, e := cmd.StdinPipe()
    40		if e != nil {
    41			t.Fatal(e)
    42		}
    43		out, e := cmd.StdoutPipe()
    44		if e != nil {
    45			t.Fatal(e)
    46		}
    47		var stderr bytes.Buffer
    48		cmd.Stderr = &stderr
    49		if e = cmd.Start(); e != nil {
    50			t.Fatal(e)
    51		}
    52		done := make(chan error, 1)
    53		go func() { done <- cmd.Wait() }()
    54		t.Cleanup(func() {
    55			_ = in.Close()
    56			select {
    57			case e := <-done:
    58				if e != nil {
    59					t.Errorf("peer exit: %v %s", e, stderr.String())
    60				}
    61			case <-time.After(3 * time.Second):
    62				_ = cmd.Process.Kill()
    63				<-done
    64				t.Error("peer did not stop")
    65			}
    66		})
    67		return &storyboardPeer{in: in, out: bufio.NewReader(out)}
    68	}
    69	func (p *storyboardPeer) call(t *testing.T, cmd any) map[string]any {
    70		t.Helper()
    71		if e := json.NewEncoder(p.in).Encode(cmd); e != nil {
    72			t.Fatal(e)
    73		}
    74		type response struct {
    75			b []byte
    76			e error
    77		}
    78		done := make(chan response, 1)
    79		go func() { b, e := p.out.ReadBytes('\n'); done <- response{b, e} }()
    80		var r response
    81		select {
    82		case r = <-done:
    83		case <-time.After(12 * time.Second):
    84			t.Fatal("peer response timeout")
    85		}
    86		if r.e != nil {
    87			t.Fatal(r.e)
    88		}
    89		var result map[string]any
    90		if e := json.Unmarshal(r.b, &result); e != nil {
    91			t.Fatal(e)
    92		}
    93		return result
    94	}
    95	func (p *storyboardPeer) ok(t *testing.T, cmd any) map[string]any {
    96		t.Helper()
    97		r := p.call(t, cmd)
    98		if r["error"] != nil {
    99			t.Fatalf("phone command failed: %v", r)
   100		}
   101		return r
   102	}
   103	func phoneLinkInvitation(t *testing.T, h *northstarPTY) phonepair.Invitation {
   104		t.Helper()
   105		h.mu.Lock()
   106		out := h.transcript.String()
   107		h.mu.Unlock()
   108		start := strings.Index(out, "wingthing://pair#v1z.")
   109		if start < 0 {
   110			t.Fatal("CLI did not display requested fallback link")
   111		}
   112		packed := strings.Fields(out[start:])[0]
   113		b, e := base64.RawURLEncoding.DecodeString(strings.TrimPrefix(packed, "wingthing://pair#v1z."))
   114		if e != nil {
   115			t.Fatal(e)
   116		}
   117		reader := flate.NewReader(bytes.NewReader(b))
   118		defer reader.Close()
   119		var parts []json.RawMessage
   120		if e = json.NewDecoder(io.LimitReader(reader, 4096)).Decode(&parts); e != nil || len(parts) != 9 {
   121			t.Fatal("invalid invitation", e)
   122		}
   123		var q phonepair.Invitation
   124		for i, target := range []any{&q.Version, &q.WingID, &q.Name, &q.Key} {
   125			if e = json.Unmarshal(parts[i], target); e != nil {
   126				t.Fatal(e)
   127			}
   128		}
   129		if e = json.Unmarshal(parts[4], &q.Local); e != nil {
   130			var endpoints []string
   131			if e = json.Unmarshal(parts[4], &endpoints); e != nil || len(endpoints) == 0 {
   132				t.Fatal("invalid direct endpoints")
   133			}
   134			q.Local, q.Endpoints = endpoints[0], endpoints[1:]
   135		}
   136		for i, target := range []any{&q.Relay, &q.ID, &q.Secret} {
   137			if e = json.Unmarshal(parts[i+5], target); e != nil {
   138				t.Fatal(e)
   139			}
   140		}
   141		var seconds int64
   142		if e = json.Unmarshal(parts[8], &seconds); e != nil {
   143			t.Fatal(e)
   144		}
   145		q.ExpiresAt = time.Unix(seconds, 0)
   146		return q
   147	}

   149	// Drive the real interactive pair command to completion. storyboardCommand
   150	// alone waits for an exit, but pairing waits for a phone and terminal approval.
   151	// The testpeer is the phone; neither a registry entry nor a CLI screen is faked.
   152	func storyboardPhoneCommand(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture, binary string, args ...string) (string, error) {
   153		t.Helper()
   154		pid, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(f.local, "wing.pid"))
   155		if err != nil {
   156			t.Fatal(err)
   157		}
   158		h := northstarStartPTY(t, f, binary, 160, 60, "pair", "--show-link")
   159		h.expect("phone fixture invitation", 0, "Pair a phone", "5:00", "Waiting for scan", "wingthing://pair#v1z.")
   160		q := phoneLinkInvitation(t, h)
   161		peer := newStoryboardPeer(t, f)
   162		peer.ok(t, map[string]any{"Op": "init", "Invitation": q, "Endpoint": q.Local})
   163		peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "start"})
   164		proof := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "prove", "Name": "Bryan's iPhone"})
   165		emoji, ok := proof["emoji"].(string)
   166		if !ok || emoji == "" {
   167			t.Fatal("phone fixture did not receive transcript-bound emoji")
   168		}
   169		h.expect("phone fixture confirmation", 0, emoji, "Bryan's iPhone wants to pair")
   170		pending := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "confirm"})
   171		if pending["state"] != "confirming" || pending["device_id"] != nil {
   172			t.Fatal("phone fixture gained access without terminal approval")
   173		}
   174		h.send("\r")
   175		h.expect("phone fixture paired", 0, "Paired Bryan's iPhone")
   176		var exitErr error
   177		select {
   178		case exitErr = <-h.done:
   179		case <-time.After(20 * time.Second):
   180			t.Fatal("phone fixture pair did not exit")
   181		}
   182		if exitErr != nil {
   183			t.Fatalf("phone fixture pair failed: %v", exitErr)
   184		}
   185		if err := h.slave.Close(); err != nil {
   186			t.Fatal(err)
   187		}
   188		select {
   189		case <-h.readDone:
   190		case <-time.After(3 * time.Second):
   191			t.Fatal("phone fixture pair PTY did not drain")
   192		}
   193		h.mu.Lock()
   194		out := h.transcript.String()
   195		h.mu.Unlock()
   196		grant := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "status"})
   197		if grant["device_id"] == nil {
   198			t.Fatal("phone fixture did not obtain a device grant")
   199		}
   200		peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "connect"})
   201		inventory := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "sessions"})
   202		rows, ok := inventory["result"].([]any)
   203		if !ok || len(rows) != 1 {
   204			t.Fatalf("phone fixture saw unexpected wing inventory: %v", inventory)
   205		}
   206		row, ok := rows[0].(map[string]any)
   207		if !ok || row["id"] != "phone-session" || row["blocked"] != true {
   208			t.Fatalf("phone fixture lost session identity/attention: %v", inventory)
   209		}
   210		peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "attach", "SessionID": "phone-session"})
   211		read := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "read", "SessionID": "phone-session"})
   212		result, ok := read["result"].(map[string]any)
   213		screen, _ := result["screen"].(string)
   214		if !ok || !strings.Contains(screen, "PHONE_SESSION_UNCHANGED") {
   215			t.Fatalf("phone fixture read unexpected session: %v", read)
   216		}
   217		peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "detach"})
   218		after, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(f.local, "wing.pid"))
   219		if err != nil || !bytes.Equal(pid, after) {
   220			t.Fatal("phone fixture pairing replaced the main wing")
   221		}
   222		if args[0] == "pair" {
   223			return out, exitErr
   224		}
   225		if args[0] == "devices" && len(args) > 1 && args[1] == "revoke" {
   226			// Card 11's unchanged prerequisite expects an unpaired-device error.
   227			// Exercise a real grant and revocation first, then retain that state for
   228			// the original command. No missing-device copy is fabricated.
   229			revokedOut, err := storyboardCommand(t, f, binary, args...)
   230			if err != nil {
   231				t.Fatalf("phone fixture revoke: %v\n%s", err, revokedOut)
   232			}
   233			storyboardContains(t, revokedOut, "Revoked Bryan's iPhone", "Live access closed")
   234			revoked := peer.call(t, map[string]string{"Op": "connect"})
   235			if revoked["error"] != "revoked" {
   236				t.Fatal("phone fixture revocation allowed reconnect", revoked)
   237			}
   238		}
   239		return storyboardCommand(t, f, binary, args...)
   240	}

   242	// This checks the repaired prerequisites separately from the original cards'
   243	// unconditional physical-device fatal assertion. It does not replace a card.
   244	func TestPhoneStoryboardHarnessPrerequisites(t *testing.T) {
   245		for _, command := range [][]string{{"pair"}, {"devices"}, {"devices", "revoke", "Bryan's iPhone"}} {
   246			t.Run(strings.Join(command, "-"), func(t *testing.T) {
   247				f, binary := storyboardFixture(t)
   248				northstarStandaloneEgg(t, f, f.local, "phone-session", "pairing", "needs_input", "PHONE_SESSION_UNCHANGED")
   249				storyboardPhoneMainWing(t, f, binary)
   250				out, err := storyboardPhoneCommand(t, f, binary, command...)
   251				switch {
   252				case command[0] == "pair":
   253					if err != nil {
   254						t.Fatal(err)
   255					}
   256					storyboardContains(t, out, "Pair a phone", "5:00", "Waiting for scan")
   257				case len(command) == 1:
   258					if err != nil {
   259						t.Fatal(err)
   260					}
   261					storyboardContains(t, out, "NAME", "PAIRED AT", "LAST SEEN", "Bryan's iPhone")
   262				default:
   263					if err == nil {
   264						t.Fatal("unpaired revoke unexpectedly succeeded")
   265					}
   266					storyboardContains(t, out, "device", "not paired")
   267				}
   268			})
   269		}
   270	}

   314			t.Fatal("tailnet IP/MagicDNS absent from main wing invitation")
   315		}

   322			t.Fatal("no transcript-bound emoji")
   323		}

   343		}

   345		if e != nil {
   346			t.Fatal(e)
   347		}
   348		storyboardContains(t, out, "NAME", "PAIRED AT", "LAST SEEN", "Bryan's iPhone")
   349		if strings.Count(out, "Bryan's iPhone") != 1 {
   350			t.Fatal("duplicate phone registry entry")
   351		}
   352		out, e = storyboardCommand(t, f, binary, "devices", "revoke", "unpaired-phone")
   353		if e == nil {
   354			t.Fatal("revoked an unpaired phone")
   355		}
   356		storyboardContains(t, out, "device", "not paired with this wing")
   357		peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "connect"})

   360		if !ok || len(rows) != 1 {
   361			t.Fatalf("wrong wing inventory: %v", inventory)
   362		}
   363		row := rows[0].(map[string]any)
   364		if row["id"] != "phone-session" || row["name"] != "pairing" || row["blocked"] != true {
   365			t.Fatalf("missing session attention: %v", row)
   366		}
   367		peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "attach", "SessionID": "phone-session"})
   368		read := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "read", "SessionID": "phone-session"})
   369		if !strings.Contains(read["result"].(map[string]any)["screen"].(string), "PHONE_SESSION_UNCHANGED") {
   370			t.Fatalf("wrong attachment screen: %v", read)
   371		}
   372		peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "input", "SessionID": "phone-session", "Input": "Run the pairing tests first.\r"})
   373		deadline := time.Now().Add(3 * time.Second)
   374		for {
   375			r := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "read", "SessionID": "phone-session"})
   376			if strings.Contains(r["result"].(map[string]any)["screen"].(string), "Run the pairing tests first.") {
   377				break
   378			}
   379			if time.Now().After(deadline) {
   380				t.Fatal("input not delivered")
   381			}
   382			time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
   383		}
   384		denied := peer.call(t, map[string]string{"Op": "config"})
   385		if denied["error"] != "denied" {
   386			t.Fatal("phone gained configuration authority", denied)
   387		}
   388		peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "detach"})
   389		if local.killed.Load() != 0 || remote.killed.Load() != 0 {
   390			t.Fatal("detach stopped a session")
   391		}
   392		stopped := northstarStandaloneEgg(t, f, f.local, "phone-stop", "stop-fixture", "working", "STOP_FIXTURE")
   393		peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "stop", "SessionID": "phone-stop"})
   394		if stopped.killed.Load() != 1 || local.killed.Load() != 0 || remote.killed.Load() != 0 {
   395			t.Fatal("phone stop affected the wrong session")
   396		}
   397		peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "attach", "SessionID": "phone-session"})
   398		out, e = storyboardCommand(t, f, binary, "devices", "revoke", "Bryan's iPhone")
   399		if e != nil {
   400			t.Fatal(e)
   401		}
   402		storyboardContains(t, out, "Revoked Bryan's iPhone", "Live access closed")
   403		revoked := peer.call(t, map[string]string{"Op": "read", "SessionID": "phone-session"})
   404		if revoked["error"] != "revoked" {
   405			t.Fatal("revocation retained live output", revoked)
   406		}
   407		revoked = peer.call(t, map[string]string{"Op": "connect"})
   408		if revoked["error"] != "revoked" {
   409			t.Fatal("revocation allowed reconnect", revoked)
   410		}

   412		if e != nil || strings.Contains(out, "Bryan's iPhone") {
   413			t.Fatal("revocation registry still active", e)
   414		}

   416		if e != nil || !bytes.Equal(pid, after) {
   417			t.Fatal("pairing replaced main daemon")
   418		}
   419		if local.killed.Load() != 0 || remote.killed.Load() != 0 {
   420			t.Fatal("pairing or revocation killed a session")
   421		}
   422		select {
   423		case <-remote.attached:
   424			t.Fatal("paired phone attached another wing")
   425		default:
   426		}
   427		// Terminal cancellation must invalidate this precise invitation without
   428		// interrupting the main wing or admitting a second device.
   429		cancelPTY := northstarStartPTY(t, f, binary, 160, 60, "pair", "--show-link")
   430		cancelPTY.expect("fresh invitation after revoke", 0, "5:00", "wingthing://pair#v1z.")
   431		fresh := phoneLinkInvitation(t, cancelPTY)
   432		if fresh.ID == q.ID || fresh.Secret == q.Secret {
   433			t.Fatal("fresh invitation reused consumed secret")
   434		}
   435		cancelPTY.send("\x03")
   436		select {
   437		case <-cancelPTY.done:
   438		case <-time.After(5 * time.Second):
   439			t.Fatal("terminal cancellation did not exit")
   440		}
   441		peer.ok(t, map[string]any{"Op": "init", "Invitation": fresh, "Endpoint": fresh.Local})
   442		cancelled := peer.call(t, map[string]string{"Op": "start"})
   443		if cancelled["error"] != "cancelled" {
   444			t.Fatal("terminal exit left invitation usable", cancelled)
   445		}

   447	}

var storyboardBuild struct {
	sync.Once
	binary string
	err    error
}

func storyboardBinary(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture) string {
	t.Helper()
	storyboardBuild.Do(func() {
		storyboardBuild.binary = filepath.Join(f.repo, ".scratch", "storyboard-wt")
		cmd := exec.CommandContext(t.Context(), "nice", "-n", "15", "go", "build", "-buildvcs=false", "-o", storyboardBuild.binary, "./cmd/wt")
		cmd.Dir = f.repo
		cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "GOFLAGS=-p=4", "GOCACHE="+filepath.Join(f.repo, ".scratch/northstar/go-build"), "GOMODCACHE="+filepath.Join(f.repo, ".scratch/northstar/go-mod"))
		if out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
			storyboardBuild.err = fmt.Errorf("build: %w\n%s", err, out)
		}
	})
	if storyboardBuild.err != nil {
		t.Fatal(storyboardBuild.err)
	}
	alias := filepath.Join(f.bin, "wt")
	if err := os.Symlink(storyboardBuild.binary, alias); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return alias
}

func storyboardFixture(t *testing.T) (*northstarFixture, string) {
	t.Helper()
	f := northstarNewFixture(t)
	f.evidence = filepath.Join(f.repo, ".scratch/storyboard-evidence", strings.ReplaceAll(t.Name(), "/", "-"))
	if err := os.MkdirAll(f.evidence, 0700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	binary := storyboardBinary(t, f)
	ssh, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(f.bin, "ssh"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	northstarWrite(t, filepath.Join(f.bin, "ssh"), strings.Replace(string(ssh), "-T|-t)", "-T|-t|--)", 1), 0700)
	return f, binary
}

func storyboardNoWork(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture) {
	t.Helper()
	for _, state := range []string{f.local, f.remote} {
		entries, err := os.ReadDir(filepath.Join(state, "eggs"))
		if err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if len(entries) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("unexpected provider/session admission in %s: %v", state, entries)
		}
		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(state, "wing.pid")); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
			t.Errorf("unexpected daemon: %s (%v)", state, err)
		}
	}
}

// Exiting commands need a PTY too. Wait for exit and drain the actual bytes;
// an idle screen is never used as evidence that the command has completed.
func storyboardCommand(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture, binary string, args ...string) (string, error) {
	t.Helper()
	h := northstarStartPTY(t, f, binary, 160, 60, args...)
	var exitErr error
	select {
	case exitErr = <-h.done:
	case <-time.After(20 * time.Second):
		t.Fatalf("command did not exit: %v", args)
	}
	if err := h.slave.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	select {
	case <-h.readDone:
	case <-time.After(3 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("PTY did not drain")
	}
	h.mu.Lock()
	out := h.transcript.String()
	fmt.Fprintf(&h.screens, "\n--- command %q; exit=%v ---\n%s\n", args, exitErr, h.emu.String())
	h.mu.Unlock()
	t.Logf("command %q exit=%v", args, exitErr)
	return out, exitErr
}

func storyboardContains(t *testing.T, output string, wants ...string) {
	t.Helper()
	flat := strings.ReplaceAll(output, "\r", "")
	for _, want := range wants {
		if !strings.Contains(flat, want) {
			t.Errorf("expected screen contains %q; actual:\n%s", want, output)
		}
	}
}

func storyboardEmpty(t *testing.T) (*northstarFixture, string, *northstarPTY) {
	t.Helper()
	f, binary := storyboardFixture(t)
	if err := os.Remove(filepath.Join(f.local, "remotes.yaml")); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { storyboardNoWork(t, f) })
	h := northstarStartPTY(t, f, binary, 140, 40)
	h.expect("empty navigator", 0, "wt |", "(no sessions)", "n new", "? help")
	return f, binary, h
}

@@ -148,0 +149,123 @@ func phoneLinkInvitation(t *testing.T, h *northstarPTY) phonepair.Invitation {
+// Drive the real interactive pair command to completion. storyboardCommand
+// alone waits for an exit, but pairing waits for a phone and terminal approval.
+// The testpeer is the phone; neither a registry entry nor a CLI screen is faked.
+func storyboardPhoneCommand(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture, binary string, args ...string) (string, error) {
+	t.Helper()
+	pid, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(f.local, "wing.pid"))
+	if err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	h := northstarStartPTY(t, f, binary, 160, 60, "pair", "--show-link")
+	h.expect("phone fixture invitation", 0, "Pair a phone", "5:00", "Waiting for scan", "wingthing://pair#v1z.")
+	q := phoneLinkInvitation(t, h)
+	peer := newStoryboardPeer(t, f)
+	peer.ok(t, map[string]any{"Op": "init", "Invitation": q, "Endpoint": q.Local})
+	peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "start"})
+	proof := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "prove", "Name": "Bryan's iPhone"})
+	emoji, ok := proof["emoji"].(string)
+	if !ok || emoji == "" {
+		t.Fatal("phone fixture did not receive transcript-bound emoji")
+	}
+	h.expect("phone fixture confirmation", 0, emoji, "Bryan's iPhone wants to pair")
+	pending := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "confirm"})
+	if pending["state"] != "confirming" || pending["device_id"] != nil {
+		t.Fatal("phone fixture gained access without terminal approval")
+	}
+	h.send("\r")
+	h.expect("phone fixture paired", 0, "Paired Bryan's iPhone")
+	var exitErr error
+	select {
+	case exitErr = <-h.done:
+	case <-time.After(20 * time.Second):
+		t.Fatal("phone fixture pair did not exit")
+	}
+	if exitErr != nil {
+		t.Fatalf("phone fixture pair failed: %v", exitErr)
+	}
+	if err := h.slave.Close(); err != nil {
+		t.Fatal(err)
+	}
+	select {
+	case <-h.readDone:
+	case <-time.After(3 * time.Second):
+		t.Fatal("phone fixture pair PTY did not drain")
+	}
+	h.mu.Lock()
+	out := h.transcript.String()
+	h.mu.Unlock()
+	grant := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "status"})
+	if grant["device_id"] == nil {
+		t.Fatal("phone fixture did not obtain a device grant")
+	}
+	peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "connect"})
+	inventory := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "sessions"})
+	rows, ok := inventory["result"].([]any)
+	if !ok || len(rows) != 1 {
+		t.Fatalf("phone fixture saw unexpected wing inventory: %v", inventory)
+	}
+	row, ok := rows[0].(map[string]any)
+	if !ok || row["id"] != "phone-session" || row["blocked"] != true {
+		t.Fatalf("phone fixture lost session identity/attention: %v", inventory)
+	}
+	peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "attach", "SessionID": "phone-session"})
+	read := peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "read", "SessionID": "phone-session"})
+	result, ok := read["result"].(map[string]any)
+	screen, _ := result["screen"].(string)
+	if !ok || !strings.Contains(screen, "PHONE_SESSION_UNCHANGED") {
+		t.Fatalf("phone fixture read unexpected session: %v", read)
+	}
+	peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "detach"})
+	after, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(f.local, "wing.pid"))
+	if err != nil || !bytes.Equal(pid, after) {
+		t.Fatal("phone fixture pairing replaced the main wing")
+	}
+	if args[0] == "pair" {
+		return out, exitErr
+	}
+	if args[0] == "devices" && len(args) > 1 && args[1] == "revoke" {
+		// Card 11's unchanged prerequisite expects an unpaired-device error.
+		// Exercise a real grant and revocation first, then retain that state for
+		// the original command. No missing-device copy is fabricated.
+		revokedOut, err := storyboardCommand(t, f, binary, args...)
+		if err != nil {
+			t.Fatalf("phone fixture revoke: %v\n%s", err, revokedOut)
+		}
+		storyboardContains(t, revokedOut, "Revoked Bryan's iPhone", "Live access closed")
+		revoked := peer.call(t, map[string]string{"Op": "connect"})
+		if revoked["error"] != "revoked" {
+			t.Fatal("phone fixture revocation allowed reconnect", revoked)
+		}
+	}
+	return storyboardCommand(t, f, binary, args...)
+}
+
+// This checks the repaired prerequisites separately from the original cards'
+// unconditional physical-device fatal assertion. It does not replace a card.
+func TestPhoneStoryboardHarnessPrerequisites(t *testing.T) {
+	for _, command := range [][]string{{"pair"}, {"devices"}, {"devices", "revoke", "Bryan's iPhone"}} {
+		t.Run(strings.Join(command, "-"), func(t *testing.T) {
+			f, binary := storyboardFixture(t)
+			northstarStandaloneEgg(t, f, f.local, "phone-session", "pairing", "needs_input", "PHONE_SESSION_UNCHANGED")
+			storyboardPhoneMainWing(t, f, binary)
+			out, err := storyboardPhoneCommand(t, f, binary, command...)
+			switch {
+			case command[0] == "pair":
+				if err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				storyboardContains(t, out, "Pair a phone", "5:00", "Waiting for scan")
+			case len(command) == 1:
+				if err != nil {
+					t.Fatal(err)
+				}
+				storyboardContains(t, out, "NAME", "PAIRED AT", "LAST SEEN", "Bryan's iPhone")
+			default:
+				if err == nil {
+					t.Fatal("unpaired revoke unexpectedly succeeded")
+				}
+				storyboardContains(t, out, "device", "not paired")
+			}
+		})
+	}
+}

     1	package main
     2	
     3	// The client mode is a disposable phone stand-in, using the same public wire
     4	// protocol as iOS. Credentials and signing keys stay in this process's memory.
     5	import (
     6		"bufio"
     7		"bytes"
     8		"crypto/cipher"
     9		"crypto/ecdh"
    10		"crypto/ecdsa"
    11		"crypto/elliptic"
    12		"crypto/rand"
    13		"crypto/sha256"
    14		"encoding/base64"
    15		"encoding/json"
    16		"errors"
    17		"fmt"
    18		"io"

    28	type phoneClient struct {
    29		q                                       phonepair.Invitation
    30		endpoint                                string
    31		signing                                 *ecdsa.PrivateKey
    32		pair, device                            cipher.AEAD
    33		emoji, deviceID, credential, connection string
    34		sequence                                uint64
    35		http                                    *http.Client
    36	}
    37	
    38	func marshal(v any) []byte { b, _ := json.Marshal(v); return b }
    39	func (p *phoneClient) wire(r phonepair.Request) (phonepair.Response, error) {
    40		var out phonepair.Response
    41		key, e := ecdh.X25519().GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
    42		if e != nil {
    43			return out, e
    44		}
    45		g, e := auth.DeriveSharedKey(key, p.q.Key, "wt-phone-carrier-v1")
    46		if e != nil {
    47			return out, e
    48		}
    49		payload, e := auth.Encrypt(g, marshal(r))
    50		if e != nil {
    51			return out, e
    52		}
    53		box := phonepair.Request{Op: "phone.box", SenderPub: base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(key.PublicKey().Bytes()), Payload: payload}
    54		resp, e := p.http.Post(p.endpoint+"/phone", "application/json", bytes.NewReader(marshal(box)))
    55		if e != nil {
    56			return out, e
    57		}
    58		defer resp.Body.Close()
    59		if resp.StatusCode != 200 {
    60			return out, fmt.Errorf("carrier status %d", resp.StatusCode)
    61		}
    62		if e = json.NewDecoder(io.LimitReader(resp.Body, phonepair.MaxPayload+4096)).Decode(&out); e != nil {
    63			return out, e
    64		}
    65		if out.Error != "" {
    66			return out, errors.New(out.Error)
    67		}
    68		b, e := auth.Decrypt(g, out.Payload)
    69		if e != nil {
    70			return out, e
    71		}
    72		if e = json.Unmarshal(b, &out); e != nil {
    73			return out, e
    74		}
    75		if out.Error != "" {
    76			return out, errors.New(out.Error)
    77		}
    78		return out, nil
    79	}
    80	func (p *phoneClient) enrollment(op string, fields map[string]any) (map[string]any, error) {
    81		fields["op"], fields["id"] = op, p.q.ID
    82		payload, e := auth.Encrypt(p.pair, marshal(fields))
    83		if e != nil {
    84			return nil, e
    85		}
    86		r, e := p.wire(phonepair.Request{Op: "pair." + op, ID: p.q.ID, Payload: payload})
    87		if e != nil {
    88			return nil, e
    89		}
    90		b, e := auth.Decrypt(p.pair, r.Payload)
    91		if e != nil {
    92			return nil, e
    93		}
    94		var result map[string]any
    95		e = json.Unmarshal(b, &result)
    96		if e != nil {
    97			return nil, e
    98		}
    99		if result["id"] != p.q.ID {
   100			return nil, errors.New("wrong invitation")
   101		}
   102		if credential, ok := result["credential"].(string); ok {
   103			id, _ := result["device_id"].(string)
   104			if result["wing_id"] != p.q.WingID || result["key"] != p.q.Key || id == "" || credential == "" {
   105				return nil, errors.New("identity_mismatch")
   106			}
   107			if p.credential != "" && (p.credential != credential || p.deviceID != id) {
   108				return nil, errors.New("duplicate grant")
   109			}
   110			p.credential, p.deviceID = credential, id
   111			delete(result, "credential")
   112		}
   113		return result, nil
   114	}
   115	func (p *phoneClient) start() (any, error) {
   116		a, am, e := pake.New(true, p.q.Secret, phonepair.Identity(p.q))
   117		if e != nil {
   118			return nil, e
   119		}
   120		defer a.Close()
   121		as := base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(am)
   122		r, e := p.wire(phonepair.Request{Op: "pair.start", ID: p.q.ID, Message: as})
   123		if e != nil {
   124			return nil, e
   125		}
   126		if r.Key != p.q.Key {
   127			return nil, errors.New("identity_mismatch")
   128		}
   129		bm, e := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(r.Message)
   130		if e != nil {
   131			return nil, e
   132		}
   133		shared, e := a.Finish(bm)
   134		if e != nil {
   135			return nil, e
   136		}
   137		p.pair, e = phonepair.PairKey(p.q, shared, as, r.Message)
   138		if e != nil {
   139			return nil, e
   140		}
   141		p.emoji = phonepair.EmojiFromShared(p.q, shared, as, r.Message)
   142		return map[string]any{"ok": true}, nil
   143	}
   144	func (p *phoneClient) connect() (any, error) {
   145		key, e := ecdh.X25519().GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
   146		if e != nil {
   147			return nil, e
   148		}
   149		pub := base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(key.PublicKey().Bytes())
   150		p.device, e = auth.DeriveSharedKey(key, p.q.Key, "wt-phone-v1")
   151		if e != nil {
   152			return nil, e
   153		}
   154		r, e := p.wire(phonepair.Request{Op: "device.hello", SenderPub: pub})
   155		if e != nil {
   156			return nil, e
   157		}
   158		b, e := auth.Decrypt(p.device, r.Payload)
   159		if e != nil {
   160			return nil, e
   161		}
   162		var hello struct {
   163			Connection string `json:"connection_id"`
   164			Challenge  string `json:"challenge"`
   165			WingID     string `json:"wing_id"`
   166			Key        string `json:"key"`
   167		}
   168		if e = json.Unmarshal(b, &hello); e != nil {
   169			return nil, e
   170		}
   171		if hello.WingID != p.q.WingID || hello.Key != p.q.Key {
   172			return nil, errors.New("identity_mismatch")
   173		}
   174		p.connection = hello.Connection
   175		p.sequence = 0
   176		digest := sha256.Sum256([]byte("wt-device-v1\n" + p.q.WingID + "\n" + p.connection + "\n" + hello.Challenge + "\n" + pub))
   177		sig, e := ecdsa.SignASN1(rand.Reader, p.signing, digest[:])
   178		if e != nil {
   179			return nil, e
   180		}
   181		payload, e := auth.Encrypt(p.device, marshal(map[string]any{"connection_id": p.connection, "device_id": p.deviceID, "credential": p.credential, "signature": base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(sig)}))
   182		if e != nil {
   183			return nil, e
   184		}
   185		r, e = p.wire(phonepair.Request{Op: "device.auth", ConnectionID: p.connection, Payload: payload})
   186		if e != nil {
   187			return nil, e
   188		}
   189		return p.deviceReply(r, 0)
   190	}
   191	func (p *phoneClient) deviceReply(r phonepair.Response, sequence uint64) (map[string]any, error) {
   192		b, e := auth.Decrypt(p.device, r.Payload)
   193		if e != nil {
   194			return nil, e
   195		}
   196		var result map[string]any
   197		if e = json.Unmarshal(b, &result); e != nil {
   198			return nil, e
   199		}
   200		if result["connection_id"] != p.connection || result["sequence"] != float64(sequence) {
   201			return nil, errors.New("response mismatch")
   202		}
   203		if code, ok := result["error"].(string); ok {
   204			return nil, errors.New(code)
   205		}
   206		return result, nil
   207	}
   208	func (p *phoneClient) rpc(op, id, input string) (any, error) {
   209		p.sequence++
   210		payload, e := auth.Encrypt(p.device, marshal(map[string]any{"op": op, "connection_id": p.connection, "sequence": p.sequence, "session_id": id, "input": input}))
   211		if e != nil {
   212			return nil, e
   213		}
   214		r, e := p.wire(phonepair.Request{Op: "device.command", ConnectionID: p.connection, Payload: payload})
   215		if e != nil {
   216			return nil, e
   217		}
   218		return p.deviceReply(r, p.sequence)
   219	}
   220	func runClient() {
   221		scanner := bufio.NewScanner(os.Stdin)
   222		scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 4096), phonepair.MaxPayload)
   223		p := &phoneClient{http: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second, Transport: &http.Transport{Proxy: nil}, CheckRedirect: func(*http.Request, []*http.Request) error { return http.ErrUseLastResponse }}}
   224		defer p.http.CloseIdleConnections()
   225		for scanner.Scan() {
   226			var cmd struct {
   227				Op, Endpoint, Name, SessionID, Input string
   228				Invitation                           phonepair.Invitation
   229			}
   230			var result any
   231			var e error
   232			if e = json.Unmarshal(scanner.Bytes(), &cmd); e == nil {
   233				switch cmd.Op {
   234				case "init":
   235					*p = phoneClient{q: cmd.Invitation, endpoint: cmd.Endpoint, http: p.http}
   236					p.signing, e = ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
   237					result = map[string]bool{"ok": e == nil}
   238				case "start":
   239					result, e = p.start()
   240				case "prove":
   241					result, e = p.enrollment("prove", map[string]any{"name": cmd.Name, "public_key": base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(elliptic.Marshal(elliptic.P256(), p.signing.X, p.signing.Y))})
   242				case "confirm":
   243					result, e = p.enrollment("confirm", map[string]any{"emoji": p.emoji})
   244				case "status", "cancel":
   245					result, e = p.enrollment(cmd.Op, map[string]any{})
   246				case "connect":
   247					result, e = p.connect()
   248				default:
   249					result, e = p.rpc(cmd.Op, cmd.SessionID, cmd.Input)
   250				}
   251			}
   252			if e != nil {
   253				result = map[string]string{"error": e.Error()}
   254			}
   255			_ = json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(result)
   256		}
   257	}

package main

// Storyboard acceptance is intentionally red for absent product requirements.
// Every journey uses the built CLI and Northstar's real kernel PTY/VT harness.
// Fixture SSH and providers never establish physical iPhone or vendor readiness.
import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/json"

var storyboardBuild struct {
	sync.Once
	binary string
	err    error
}

func storyboardBinary(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture) string {
	t.Helper()
	storyboardBuild.Do(func() {
		storyboardBuild.binary = filepath.Join(f.repo, ".scratch", "storyboard-wt")
		cmd := exec.CommandContext(t.Context(), "nice", "-n", "15", "go", "build", "-buildvcs=false", "-o", storyboardBuild.binary, "./cmd/wt")
		cmd.Dir = f.repo
		cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(), "GOFLAGS=-p=4", "GOCACHE="+filepath.Join(f.repo, ".scratch/northstar/go-build"), "GOMODCACHE="+filepath.Join(f.repo, ".scratch/northstar/go-mod"))
		if out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
			storyboardBuild.err = fmt.Errorf("build: %w\n%s", err, out)
		}
	})
	if storyboardBuild.err != nil {
		t.Fatal(storyboardBuild.err)
	}
	alias := filepath.Join(f.bin, "wt")
	if err := os.Symlink(storyboardBuild.binary, alias); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return alias
}

func storyboardFixture(t *testing.T) (*northstarFixture, string) {
	t.Helper()
	f := northstarNewFixture(t)
	f.evidence = filepath.Join(f.repo, ".scratch/storyboard-evidence", strings.ReplaceAll(t.Name(), "/", "-"))
	if err := os.MkdirAll(f.evidence, 0700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	binary := storyboardBinary(t, f)
	ssh, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(f.bin, "ssh"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	northstarWrite(t, filepath.Join(f.bin, "ssh"), strings.Replace(string(ssh), "-T|-t)", "-T|-t|--)", 1), 0700)
	return f, binary
}

func storyboardNoWork(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture) {
	t.Helper()
	for _, state := range []string{f.local, f.remote} {
		entries, err := os.ReadDir(filepath.Join(state, "eggs"))
		if err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if len(entries) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("unexpected provider/session admission in %s: %v", state, entries)
		}
		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(state, "wing.pid")); !os.IsNotExist(err) {
			t.Errorf("unexpected daemon: %s (%v)", state, err)
		}
	}
}

// Exiting commands need a PTY too. Wait for exit and drain the actual bytes;
// an idle screen is never used as evidence that the command has completed.
func storyboardCommand(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture, binary string, args ...string) (string, error) {
	t.Helper()
	h := northstarStartPTY(t, f, binary, 160, 60, args...)
	var exitErr error
	select {
	case exitErr = <-h.done:
	case <-time.After(20 * time.Second):
		t.Fatalf("command did not exit: %v", args)
	}
	if err := h.slave.Close(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	select {
	case <-h.readDone:
	case <-time.After(3 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("PTY did not drain")
	}
	h.mu.Lock()
	out := h.transcript.String()
	fmt.Fprintf(&h.screens, "\n--- command %q; exit=%v ---\n%s\n", args, exitErr, h.emu.String())
	h.mu.Unlock()
	t.Logf("command %q exit=%v", args, exitErr)
	return out, exitErr
}

func storyboardContains(t *testing.T, output string, wants ...string) {
	t.Helper()
	flat := strings.ReplaceAll(output, "\r", "")
	for _, want := range wants {
		if !strings.Contains(flat, want) {
			t.Errorf("expected screen contains %q; actual:\n%s", want, output)
		}
	}
}
			t.Fatal("input not delivered")
		}
		time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
	}
	denied := peer.call(t, map[string]string{"Op": "config"})
	if denied["error"] != "denied" {
		t.Fatal("phone gained configuration authority", denied)
	}
	peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "detach"})
	if local.killed.Load() != 0 || remote.killed.Load() != 0 {
		t.Fatal("detach stopped a session")
	}
	stopped := northstarStandaloneEgg(t, f, f.local, "phone-stop", "stop-fixture", "working", "STOP_FIXTURE")
	peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "stop", "SessionID": "phone-stop"})
	if stopped.killed.Load() != 1 || local.killed.Load() != 0 || remote.killed.Load() != 0 {
		t.Fatal("phone stop affected the wrong session")
	}
	peer.ok(t, map[string]string{"Op": "attach", "SessionID": "phone-session"})
	out, e = storyboardCommand(t, f, binary, "devices", "revoke", "Bryan's iPhone")
	if e != nil {
		t.Fatal(e)
	}
	storyboardContains(t, out, "Revoked Bryan's iPhone", "Live access closed")
	revoked := peer.call(t, map[string]string{"Op": "read", "SessionID": "phone-session"})
	if revoked["error"] != "revoked" {
		t.Fatal("revocation retained live output", revoked)
	}
	revoked = peer.call(t, map[string]string{"Op": "connect"})
	if revoked["error"] != "revoked" {
		t.Fatal("revocation allowed reconnect", revoked)
	}
	out, e = storyboardCommand(t, f, binary, "devices")
	if e != nil || strings.Contains(out, "Bryan's iPhone") {
		t.Fatal("revocation registry still active", e)
	}
	after, e := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(f.local, "wing.pid"))
	if e != nil || !bytes.Equal(pid, after) {
		t.Fatal("pairing replaced main daemon")
	}
	if local.killed.Load() != 0 || remote.killed.Load() != 0 {
		t.Fatal("pairing or revocation killed a session")
	}
	select {
	case <-remote.attached:
		t.Fatal("paired phone attached another wing")
	default:
	}
	// Terminal cancellation must invalidate this precise invitation without
	// interrupting the main wing or admitting a second device.
	cancelPTY := northstarStartPTY(t, f, binary, 160, 60, "pair", "--show-link")
	cancelPTY.expect("fresh invitation after revoke", 0, "5:00", "wingthing://pair#v1z.")
	fresh := phoneLinkInvitation(t, cancelPTY)
	if fresh.ID == q.ID || fresh.Secret == q.Secret {
		t.Fatal("fresh invitation reused consumed secret")
	}
	cancelPTY.send("\x03")
	select {
	case <-cancelPTY.done:
	case <-time.After(5 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("terminal cancellation did not exit")
	}
	peer.ok(t, map[string]any{"Op": "init", "Invitation": fresh, "Endpoint": fresh.Local})
	cancelled := peer.call(t, map[string]string{"Op": "start"})
	if cancelled["error"] != "cancelled" {
		t.Fatal("terminal exit left invitation usable", cancelled)
	}
	t.Log("05 cards 0–6, 11–12, 17: CLI invitation, two approvals, recovery, inventory, attach/input/detach and live revoke PASS; physical phone checks REQUIRED-UNRUN")
}

package main

// Shared, test-only kernel PTY helpers for the Northstar acceptance journeys.
// Providers and SSH may be fixtures; the root CLI and terminal handoff are real.
import (
	"bufio"
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"path/filepath"
	"reflect"
	"runtime"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"sync/atomic"
	"syscall"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/charmbracelet/x/vt"

// Only the test executable recognizes this fixture switch. No production CLI
// backdoor or provider is needed to hold an owned process for standalone eggs.
func init() {
	if os.Getenv("WT_NORTHSTAR_PID_FIXTURE") == "1" {
		_, _ = io.WriteString(os.Stdout, "ready\n")
		_, _ = io.Copy(io.Discard, os.Stdin)
		os.Exit(0)
	}
}

type northstarFixture struct {
	repo, root, home, local, remote, work, tmp, bin, evidence string
	env                                                       []string
}

func northstarWrite(t *testing.T, path, content string, perm os.FileMode) {
	t.Helper()
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(content), perm); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func northstarNewFixture(t *testing.T) *northstarFixture {
	t.Helper()
	repo, err := filepath.Abs("../..")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	scratch := filepath.Join(repo, ".scratch")
	if err := os.MkdirAll(scratch, 0700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	root, err := os.MkdirTemp(scratch, "northstar-")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = os.RemoveAll(root) })
	home, local, remote := testprovider.ScopedHomeDirs(t, scratch)
	f := &northstarFixture{repo: repo, root: root, home: home, local: local, remote: remote,
		work: filepath.Join(root, "work"), tmp: filepath.Join(root, "tmp-alias"), bin: filepath.Join(root, "tmp-alias", "bin"),
		evidence: filepath.Join(scratch, "northstar", "northstar-evidence")}
	// TMPDIR must actually traverse an alias created by this regression. State
	// and mailbox stay in protected HOME, outside provider-writable work/TMPDIR.
	if err := os.Mkdir(filepath.Join(root, "tmp-real"), 0700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.Symlink(filepath.Join(root, "tmp-real"), f.tmp); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, dir := range []string{f.work, f.bin, filepath.Join(home, "mailbox"), f.evidence} {
		if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0700); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	f.env = []string{"HOME=" + home, "WINGTHING_DIR=" + local, "PATH=" + f.bin + ":/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin", "TMPDIR=" + f.tmp,
		"TERM=xterm-256color", "LANG=en_US.UTF-8", "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1", "NORTHSTAR_SSH_OFFLINE_FILE=" + filepath.Join(home, "forge-offline")}
	// Only these exact fixture aliases can reach the shell executor. The command
	// still runs the built wt with the literal state/argv produced by real SSH code.
	northstarWrite(t, filepath.Join(f.bin, "ssh"), `#!/bin/sh
while [ "$#" -gt 0 ]; do
 case "$1" in -T|-t) shift ;; -o) shift 2 ;; *) break ;; esac
done
host=$1
shift
case "$host" in
 ns-forge)
 if [ -f "$NORTHSTAR_SSH_OFFLINE_FILE" ]; then printf 'fixture query failure\n' >&2; exit 255; fi
 exec /bin/sh -c "$1" ;;
 ns-empty) exec /bin/sh -c "$1" ;;
 ns-offline) printf 'fixture offline\n' >&2; exit 255 ;;
 *) printf 'unexpected fixture SSH target\n' >&2; exit 99 ;;
esac
`, 0700)
	empty := filepath.Join(home, "e")
	if err := os.Mkdir(empty, 0700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	northstarWrite(t, filepath.Join(local, "remotes.yaml"), fmt.Sprintf(`remotes:
  forge:
    ssh_target: ns-forge
    wingthing_dir: %s
  empty:
    ssh_target: ns-empty
    wingthing_dir: %s
  offline:
    ssh_target: ns-offline
`, remote, empty), 0600)
	return f
}

		}
	}
}

func northstarStandaloneEgg(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture, state, id, name, lifecycle, marker string) *northstarEgg {
	t.Helper()
	dir := filepath.Join(state, "eggs", id)
	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	exe, err := os.Executable()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	identity := exec.CommandContext(t.Context(), exe, "egg", "run", "--session-id", id)
	identity.Env = append(f.env, "WT_NORTHSTAR_PID_FIXTURE=1")
	input, err := identity.StdinPipe()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	ready, err := identity.StdoutPipe()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := identity.Start(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { done <- identity.Wait() }()
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		_ = input.Close()
		select {
		case <-done:
		case <-time.After(2 * time.Second):
			_ = identity.Process.Kill()
			<-done
		}
	})
	ack := make(chan string, 1)
	go func() { line, _ := bufio.NewReader(ready).ReadString('\n'); ack <- line }()
	select {
	case line := <-ack:
		if line != "ready\n" {
			t.Fatalf("fixture identity: %q", line)
		}
	case <-time.After(3 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("fixture identity timeout")
	}
	for file, value := range map[string]string{"egg.pid": strconv.Itoa(identity.Process.Pid), "egg.token": "northstar-fixture-token",
		"egg.meta":        "kind=agent\nagent=claude\ncwd=" + f.work + "\nisolation=fixture\n",
		"lifecycle.jsonl": fmt.Sprintf("{\"sequence\":1,\"type\":\"session_ready\",\"source\":\"claude_hook\",\"state\":%q}\n", lifecycle)} {
		northstarWrite(t, filepath.Join(dir, file), value, 0600)
	}
	if err := eggclient.WriteSessionName(dir, name); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	listener, err := net.Listen("unix", northstarSocket(t, filepath.Join(dir, "egg.sock")))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	fixture := &northstarEgg{dir: dir, marker: marker, attached: make(chan string, 4)}
	rpc := grpc.NewServer()
	pb.RegisterEggServer(rpc, fixture)
	serveDone := make(chan struct{})
	go func() { defer close(serveDone); _ = rpc.Serve(listener) }()
	t.Cleanup(func() { rpc.Stop(); _ = listener.Close(); <-serveDone })
	return fixture
}

type northstarPTY struct {
	t             *testing.T
	cmd           *exec.Cmd
	master, slave *os.File
	before        *term.State
	emu           *vt.Emulator
	mu            sync.Mutex
	revision      uint64
	changed       chan struct{}
	done          chan error
	readDone      chan struct{}
	transcript    bytes.Buffer
	screens       strings.Builder
	artifact      string
}

func northstarStartPTY(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture, binary string, cols, rows int, args ...string) *northstarPTY {
	t.Helper()
	master, slave, err := pty.Open()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	before, err := term.GetState(int(slave.Fd()))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := pty.Setsize(master, &pty.Winsize{Cols: uint16(cols), Rows: uint16(rows)}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cmd := exec.CommandContext(t.Context(), binary, args...)
	cmd.Env, cmd.Dir = f.env, f.work
	cmd.Stdin, cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = slave, slave, slave
	cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{Setsid: true, Setctty: true, Ctty: 0}
	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h := &northstarPTY{t: t, cmd: cmd, master: master, slave: slave, before: before, emu: vt.NewEmulator(cols, rows), changed: make(chan struct{}, 1), done: make(chan error, 1), readDone: make(chan struct{}), artifact: filepath.Join(f.evidence, strings.ReplaceAll(t.Name(), "/", "-")+"-"+strconv.Itoa(cols)+"-"+strconv.Itoa(cmd.Process.Pid))}
	// VT query replies must be drained even if a child asks for capabilities.
	queryDone := make(chan struct{})
	inputPipe := h.emu.InputPipe().(io.Closer)
	go func() { defer close(queryDone); _, _ = io.Copy(io.Discard, h.emu) }()
	go func() { h.done <- cmd.Wait(); close(h.done) }()
	go func() {
		defer close(h.readDone)
		buf := make([]byte, 8192)
		for {
			n, err := master.Read(buf)
			if n > 0 {
				h.mu.Lock()
				h.transcript.Write(buf[:n])
				_, _ = h.emu.Write(buf[:n])
				h.revision++
				h.mu.Unlock()
				select {
				case h.changed <- struct{}{}:
				default:
				}
			}
			if err != nil {
				return
			}
		}
	}()
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		// Exact owned child group only; no process scans or state-derived PID kills.
		select {
		case <-h.done:
		default:
			_ = syscall.Kill(-cmd.Process.Pid, syscall.SIGTERM)
			select {
			case <-h.done:
			case <-time.After(2 * time.Second):
				_ = syscall.Kill(-cmd.Process.Pid, syscall.SIGKILL)
				select {
				case <-h.done:
				case <-time.After(time.Second):
					t.Error("owned PTY child did not join")
				}
			}
		}
		_ = master.Close()
		_ = slave.Close()
		<-h.readDone
		// Emulator.Close changes its unsynchronized closed flag. Close the
		// thread-safe pipe first and join the query reader before that change.
		_ = inputPipe.Close()
		<-queryDone
		h.mu.Lock()
		defer h.mu.Unlock()
		_ = h.emu.Close()
		northstarWrite(t, h.artifact+".ansi", h.transcript.String(), 0600)
		northstarWrite(t, h.artifact+".txt", h.screens.String(), 0600)
		t.Logf("PTY screen transcript: %s.txt", h.artifact)
	})
	return h
}

func (h *northstarPTY) send(input string) uint64 {
	h.t.Helper()
	h.mu.Lock()
	rev := h.revision
	h.mu.Unlock()
	if _, err := io.WriteString(h.master, input); err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return rev
}
func (h *northstarPTY) screen() (string, uint64) {
	h.mu.Lock()
	defer h.mu.Unlock()
	return h.emu.String(), h.revision
}
func (h *northstarPTY) waitScreen(label string, after uint64, match func(string) bool) string {
	h.t.Helper()
	timer := time.NewTimer(8 * time.Second)
	defer timer.Stop()
	for {
		screen, rev := h.screen()
		if rev > after && match(screen) {
			h.mu.Lock()
			fmt.Fprintf(&h.screens, "\n--- %s ---\n%s\n", label, screen)
			h.mu.Unlock()
			return screen
		}
		select {
		case <-h.changed:
		case <-timer.C:
			h.t.Fatalf("screen timeout (%s):\n%s", label, screen)
		}
	}
}
func (h *northstarPTY) expect(label string, after uint64, contains ...string) string {
	h.t.Helper()
	return h.waitScreen(label, after, func(screen string) bool {
		flat := strings.NewReplacer("\r", "", "\n", "").Replace(screen)
		for _, text := range contains {
			if !strings.Contains(flat, text) {
				return false
			}
		}
		return true
	})
}
func (h *northstarPTY) resize(cols, rows int) uint64 {
	h.t.Helper()
	h.mu.Lock()
	rev := h.revision
	h.emu.Resize(cols, rows)
	h.mu.Unlock()
	if err := pty.Setsize(h.master, &pty.Winsize{Cols: uint16(cols), Rows: uint16(rows)}); err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := h.cmd.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGWINCH); err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return rev
}
func (h *northstarPTY) exit(input string) {
	h.t.Helper()
	h.send(input)
	select {
	case err := <-h.done:
		if err != nil {
			h.t.Fatalf("PTY child exit: %v", err)
		}
	case <-time.After(5 * time.Second):
		h.t.Fatal("PTY child exit timeout")
	}
	// Darwin revokes the slave when its session leader exits; the master
	// retains the shared terminal attributes for the restoration assertion.
	after, err := term.GetState(int(h.master.Fd()))
	if err != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(h.before, after) {
		h.t.Fatalf("cooked termios not restored: before=%+v after=%+v err=%v", h.before, after, err)
	}
}

func TestNorthstarPTYSessionNavigator(t *testing.T) {

exec
/bin/bash -lc "rg -n 'main wing|wt wing start|CGO|cgo|two-sided|confirmation|Go|REQUIRED|physical|revok|Keychain|Invitation|local control' docs/pairing/PROTOCOL.md; rg -n 'func|revok|paired|LocalOnly|wing.pid|Phone|pairing' cmd/wt/phone.go cmd/wt/wing.go internal/wing/phone.go" in /work/lanes/wt-phone-harness/wingthing
 succeeded in 0ms:
3:The contract is `/Users/ehrlich/repos/claude/state/drive/wingthing-pairing-spec.md` (11 October 2026). Pair Bryan's iPhone to **EhrlichMac's main wing** first; pair **forge's wing** separately. A phone grant permits session inventory, attachment, input and stop on precisely one wing. It confers no account, configuration, enrollment, shell-launch or other-machine authority. `wt pair` must contact the running wing's local control endpoint, not start a second wing with a new identity.
9:Use **BoringSSL's SPAKE2 implementation**, not a new PAKE implementation. Vendor the symbol-prefixed source shipped by Apple's Swift Crypto at revision `1c80d3aff53fedc445b60692980cb4f19a618657`, derived from BoringSSL `0226f30467f540a3f62ef48d453f93927da199b6`. Go and Swift call the same `SPAKE2_CTX_new`, `SPAKE2_generate_msg` and `SPAKE2_process_msg` library APIs. This is BoringSSL's Ed25519 SPAKE2 dialect; do not claim wire compatibility with arbitrary RFC 9382 implementations. Each context is used once and freed. BoringSSL is a maintained cryptographic library used by Chromium and Swift Crypto; this integration still needs independent review before a public security claim.
11:Primary references: [BoringSSL SPAKE2 API](https://commondatastorage.googleapis.com/chromium-boringssl-docs/curve25519.h.html), [Swift Crypto source](https://github.com/apple/swift-crypto/tree/1c80d3aff53fedc445b60692980cb4f19a618657), [BoringSSL security policy](https://github.com/google/boringssl/blob/main/SECURITY.md). AES-256-GCM, X25519, HKDF-SHA256, HMAC-SHA256 and P-256 signing use Go standard crypto / x/crypto and Apple CryptoKit. No curve arithmetic, password exchange or cipher is implemented here.
13:## Invitation and transport
15:The wing already has a persistent X25519 `wing_key` and WingID. Its SHA-256 fingerprint is the full lowercase hex digest of the decoded 32-byte public key. The QR is `wingthing://pair#v1z.` followed by unpadded base64url of RFC 1951 raw-DEFLATE compression (Go `compress/flate`, Apple Foundation `NSData` zlib API) of a compact JSON array in this exact order: `[v, wing_id, name, key, local, relay, id, secret, expires_unix_seconds]`. Compressing the isolated invitation keeps the real QR within ordinary terminal widths even with tailnet endpoints. This compresses only a locally displayed invitation, never credential/session traffic; the input QR is limited to 4 KiB and decompressed JSON to 4 KiB. The app also accepts uncompressed `v1.` arrays. When there are additional direct endpoints, field `local` in this array is an ordered array `[primary_lan, other_lan, tailnet_ip, magicdns]` instead of one string. The expanded form retains `local` plus optional `endpoints`. The equivalent expanded object below describes the fields; the app also accepts percent-encoded expanded JSON after `wingthing://pair#` for the hidden paste fallback:
25:Local-only is the default and requires no Wingthing account or payment. A personal wing with no valid registration starts locally with plain `wt wing start`; no extra flag, relay lookup, account login or relay transport is required. Even a saved relay URL cannot opt an accountless wing into relay machinery. To keep a registered wing entirely local, use `wt wing start --local-only`. The default setup is:
28:wt wing start   # if the main wing is not already running
38:## PAKE and two-sided confirmation
40:All binary fields below are standard padded base64. Transport requests are JSON objects; errors are stable codes (`expired`, `used`, `cancelled`, `identity_mismatch`, `bad_proof`, `revoked`, `unreachable`, `denied`). Do not reflect secrets in errors or logs.
50:Generate a persistent P-256 signing key per pairing on the phone. Use Secure Enclave when supported, otherwise CryptoKit with a non-synchronizing, `kSecAttrAccessibleWhenUnlockedThisDeviceOnly` Keychain item. Store the opaque device credential and key representation together in a device-only Keychain item scoped by WingID, pinned wing key and device ID. A Secure Enclave representation wraps a hardware-bound key; it is not the private scalar. Public machine names, endpoints, pins and device IDs may be stored in Application Support. Never place a secret or private key in UserDefaults, profile JSON, setup URLs, diagnostics or the clipboard. Each pairing is independent. X25519 connection keys are ephemeral and remain in memory.
52:For each connection, phone creates ephemeral X25519 and derives AES-GCM to the scanned persistent wing key with the existing `wt-phone-v1` HKDF domain (32 zero salt bytes). `device.hello {sender_pub}` returns an encrypted random challenge and random connection ID. The phone signs UTF-8 `wt-device-v1\n<wing_id>\n<connection_id>\n<challenge>\n<sender_pub>` with its stored P-256 key. It sends encrypted `device.auth {device_id, credential, signature, connection_id}`. The wing checks credential hash in constant time, signature, fresh challenge, live device record and exact wing binding. The encrypted hello also returns current direct endpoints and the registered relay origin; after successful device authentication the app saves that public metadata. The existing `wt login` command restarts a running main wing with its new registration; this login-specific restart removes `--local-only` while retaining the same state directory, wing key, roost and session configuration. Ordinary restarts preserve local-only mode. Following `wt login` on a previously local-only Mac, reconnect the app once on the LAN so it learns the relay origin, without re-pairing or logging in on the phone. Challenge is single-use, expires in 30 seconds and authentication failure closes the connection.
70:`wt devices` talks to the same main wing over local authenticated control. It lists paired phone name, paired-at and last seen. `wt devices revoke <name>` resolves exactly one active name; ambiguous names require the device ID. Under the same registry lock that admits commands, persist revocation and cancel all connections/attachments for that device before acknowledging success. New commands fail `revoked`; a relay cannot revive a revoked credential. Re-pairing requires a new QR and both confirmations. On loss, revoke separately on EhrlichMac and forge. Cached content on a lost phone cannot be remotely erased by this protocol.
84:A machine name is a display label, never an authorization selector. The persistent key and WingID identify the wing. A malicious QR can name a different machine “EhrlichMac”; Bryan's trusted terminal and two-sided check are the out-of-band authority. Never claim simulator acceptance proves a real QR camera scan, Keychain/Secure Enclave behavior on Bryan's hardware, or cellular reachability. Those remain required physical-phone checks.
88:The entire protocol above, including PAKE public messages, name/key replies and errors, is carried inside a second X25519/AES-GCM envelope to the scanned wing key. Before local HTTP or relay forwarding, generate a per-wire ephemeral X25519 key and derive with domain `wt-phone-carrier-v1`. Send `phone.box {sender_pub, payload}`; `payload` encrypts the exact request described above. The wing decrypts, processes the request and encrypts its complete response under that carrier key. Thus the relay sees only the routing ID, random ephemeral public key, request-correlation ID and ciphertext; it cannot inspect enrollment state, phone/machine names, PAKE messages, credentials or errors. PAKE authentication and both human confirmations remain mandatory inside this envelope. Changing carrier is allowed only for initial `pair.start`/`device.hello`, never after an uncertain session command.
92:Pairing requires a native CGO build and C++17 compiler because both apps call the same vetted library. On EhrlichMac or forge, build `CGO_ENABLED=1 go build -o wt ./cmd/wt` using Go 1.26.9 and a native C++ compiler (Clang on macOS, `g++` on Linux). The existing `CGO_ENABLED=0` distribution builds retain the previous CLI/wing behavior and explicitly reject phone pairing; they do not replace PAKE with an unsafe implementation. Native iOS SwiftPM compiles the same portable BoringSSL sources. A Linux CGO build remains a separate platform check; no binary was installed or daemon restarted in this task.
