	"github.com/creack/pty"
	"github.com/ehrlich-b/wingthing/internal/controlsocket"
	pb "github.com/ehrlich-b/wingthing/internal/egg/pb"
	"github.com/ehrlich-b/wingthing/internal/eggclient"
	"github.com/ehrlich-b/wingthing/internal/testprovider"
	"golang.org/x/term"
	"google.golang.org/grpc"
)

// Only the test executable recognizes this fixture switch. No production CLI
// backdoor or provider is needed to hold an owned process for standalone eggs.
func init() {
	if os.Getenv("WT_NORTHSTAR_PID_FIXTURE") == "1" {
		_, _ = io.WriteString(os.Stdout, "ready\n")
		_, _ = io.Copy(io.Discard, os.Stdin)
		os.Exit(0)
	}
}

type northstarFixture struct {
	repo, root, home, local, remote, work, tmp, bin, evidence string
	env                                                       []string
}

func northstarWrite(t *testing.T, path, content string, perm os.FileMode) {
	t.Helper()
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(content), perm); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

func northstarNewFixture(t *testing.T) *northstarFixture {
	t.Helper()
	repo, err := filepath.Abs("../..")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	scratch := filepath.Join(repo, ".scratch")
	if err := os.MkdirAll(scratch, 0700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	root, err := os.MkdirTemp(scratch, "northstar-")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = os.RemoveAll(root) })
	home, local, remote := testprovider.ScopedHomeDirs(t, scratch)
	f := &northstarFixture{repo: repo, root: root, home: home, local: local, remote: remote,
		work: filepath.Join(root, "work"), tmp: filepath.Join(root, "tmp-alias"), bin: filepath.Join(root, "tmp-alias", "bin"),
		evidence: filepath.Join(scratch, "northstar", "northstar-evidence")}
	// TMPDIR must actually traverse an alias created by this regression. State
	// and mailbox stay in protected HOME, outside provider-writable work/TMPDIR.
	if err := os.Mkdir(filepath.Join(root, "tmp-real"), 0700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.Symlink(filepath.Join(root, "tmp-real"), f.tmp); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, dir := range []string{f.work, f.bin, filepath.Join(home, "mailbox"), f.evidence} {
		if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0700); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	f.env = []string{"HOME=" + home, "WINGTHING_DIR=" + local, "PATH=" + f.bin + ":/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin", "TMPDIR=" + f.tmp,
		"TERM=xterm-256color", "LANG=en_US.UTF-8", "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1", "NORTHSTAR_SSH_OFFLINE_FILE=" + filepath.Join(home, "forge-offline")}
	// Only these exact fixture aliases can reach the shell executor. The command
	// still runs the built wt with the literal state/argv produced by real SSH code.
	northstarWrite(t, filepath.Join(f.bin, "ssh"), `#!/bin/sh
while [ "$#" -gt 0 ]; do
 case "$1" in -T|-t) shift ;; -o) shift 2 ;; *) break ;; esac
done
host=$1
shift
case "$host" in
 ns-forge)
 if [ -f "$NORTHSTAR_SSH_OFFLINE_FILE" ]; then printf 'fixture query failure\n' >&2; exit 255; fi
 exec /bin/sh -c "$1" ;;
 ns-empty) exec /bin/sh -c "$1" ;;
 ns-offline) printf 'fixture offline\n' >&2; exit 255 ;;
 *) printf 'unexpected fixture SSH target\n' >&2; exit 99 ;;
esac
`, 0700)
	empty := filepath.Join(home, "e")
	if err := os.Mkdir(empty, 0700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	northstarWrite(t, filepath.Join(local, "remotes.yaml"), fmt.Sprintf(`remotes:
  forge:
    ssh_target: ns-forge
    wingthing_dir: %s
  empty:
    ssh_target: ns-empty
    wingthing_dir: %s
  offline:
    ssh_target: ns-offline
`, remote, empty), 0600)
	return f
}

		}
	}
}

func northstarStandaloneEgg(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture, state, id, name, lifecycle, marker string) *northstarEgg {
	t.Helper()
	dir := filepath.Join(state, "eggs", id)
	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	exe, err := os.Executable()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	identity := exec.CommandContext(t.Context(), exe, "egg", "run", "--session-id", id)
	identity.Env = append(f.env, "WT_NORTHSTAR_PID_FIXTURE=1")
	input, err := identity.StdinPipe()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	ready, err := identity.StdoutPipe()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := identity.Start(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { done <- identity.Wait() }()
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		_ = input.Close()
		select {
		case <-done:
		case <-time.After(2 * time.Second):
			_ = identity.Process.Kill()
			<-done
		}
	})
	ack := make(chan string, 1)
	go func() { line, _ := bufio.NewReader(ready).ReadString('\n'); ack <- line }()
	select {
	case line := <-ack:
		if line != "ready\n" {
			t.Fatalf("fixture identity: %q", line)
		}
	case <-time.After(3 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("fixture identity timeout")
	}
	for file, value := range map[string]string{"egg.pid": strconv.Itoa(identity.Process.Pid), "egg.token": "northstar-fixture-token",
		"egg.meta":        "kind=agent\nagent=claude\ncwd=" + f.work + "\nisolation=fixture\n",
		"lifecycle.jsonl": fmt.Sprintf("{\"sequence\":1,\"type\":\"session_ready\",\"source\":\"claude_hook\",\"state\":%q}\n", lifecycle)} {
		northstarWrite(t, filepath.Join(dir, file), value, 0600)
	}
	if err := eggclient.WriteSessionName(dir, name); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	listener, err := net.Listen("unix", northstarSocket(t, filepath.Join(dir, "egg.sock")))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	fixture := &northstarEgg{dir: dir, marker: marker, attached: make(chan string, 4)}
	rpc := grpc.NewServer()
	pb.RegisterEggServer(rpc, fixture)
	serveDone := make(chan struct{})
	go func() { defer close(serveDone); _ = rpc.Serve(listener) }()
	t.Cleanup(func() { rpc.Stop(); _ = listener.Close(); <-serveDone })
	return fixture
}

type northstarPTY struct {
	t             *testing.T
	cmd           *exec.Cmd
	master, slave *os.File
	before        *term.State
	emu           *vt.Emulator
	mu            sync.Mutex
	revision      uint64
	changed       chan struct{}
	done          chan error
	readDone      chan struct{}
	transcript    bytes.Buffer
	screens       strings.Builder
	artifact      string
}

func northstarStartPTY(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture, binary string, cols, rows int, args ...string) *northstarPTY {
	t.Helper()
	master, slave, err := pty.Open()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	before, err := term.GetState(int(slave.Fd()))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := pty.Setsize(master, &pty.Winsize{Cols: uint16(cols), Rows: uint16(rows)}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cmd := exec.CommandContext(t.Context(), binary, args...)
	cmd.Env, cmd.Dir = f.env, f.work
	cmd.Stdin, cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = slave, slave, slave
	cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{Setsid: true, Setctty: true, Ctty: 0}
	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h := &northstarPTY{t: t, cmd: cmd, master: master, slave: slave, before: before, emu: vt.NewEmulator(cols, rows), changed: make(chan struct{}, 1), done: make(chan error, 1), readDone: make(chan struct{}), artifact: filepath.Join(f.evidence, strings.ReplaceAll(t.Name(), "/", "-")+"-"+strconv.Itoa(cols)+"-"+strconv.Itoa(cmd.Process.Pid))}
	// VT query replies must be drained even if a child asks for capabilities.
	queryDone := make(chan struct{})
	inputPipe := h.emu.InputPipe().(io.Closer)
	go func() { defer close(queryDone); _, _ = io.Copy(io.Discard, h.emu) }()
	go func() { h.done <- cmd.Wait(); close(h.done) }()
	go func() {
		defer close(h.readDone)
		buf := make([]byte, 8192)
		for {
			n, err := master.Read(buf)
			if n > 0 {
				h.mu.Lock()
				h.transcript.Write(buf[:n])
				_, _ = h.emu.Write(buf[:n])
				h.revision++
				h.mu.Unlock()
				select {
				case h.changed <- struct{}{}:
				default:
				}
			}
			if err != nil {
				return
			}
		}
	}()
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		// Exact owned child group only; no process scans or state-derived PID kills.
		select {
		case <-h.done:
		default:
			_ = syscall.Kill(-cmd.Process.Pid, syscall.SIGTERM)
			select {
			case <-h.done:
			case <-time.After(2 * time.Second):
				_ = syscall.Kill(-cmd.Process.Pid, syscall.SIGKILL)
				select {
				case <-h.done:
				case <-time.After(time.Second):
					t.Error("owned PTY child did not join")
				}
			}
		}
		_ = master.Close()
		_ = slave.Close()
		<-h.readDone
		// Emulator.Close changes its unsynchronized closed flag. Close the
		// thread-safe pipe first and join the query reader before that change.
		_ = inputPipe.Close()
		<-queryDone
		h.mu.Lock()
		defer h.mu.Unlock()
		_ = h.emu.Close()
		northstarWrite(t, h.artifact+".ansi", h.transcript.String(), 0600)
		northstarWrite(t, h.artifact+".txt", h.screens.String(), 0600)
		t.Logf("PTY screen transcript: %s.txt", h.artifact)
	})
	return h
}

func (h *northstarPTY) send(input string) uint64 {
	h.t.Helper()
	h.mu.Lock()
	rev := h.revision
	h.mu.Unlock()
	if _, err := io.WriteString(h.master, input); err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return rev
}
func (h *northstarPTY) screen() (string, uint64) {
	h.mu.Lock()
	defer h.mu.Unlock()
	return h.emu.String(), h.revision
}
func (h *northstarPTY) waitScreen(label string, after uint64, match func(string) bool) string {
	h.t.Helper()
	timer := time.NewTimer(8 * time.Second)
	defer timer.Stop()
	for {
		screen, rev := h.screen()
		if rev > after && match(screen) {
			h.mu.Lock()
			fmt.Fprintf(&h.screens, "\n--- %s ---\n%s\n", label, screen)
			h.mu.Unlock()
			return screen
		}
		select {
		case <-h.changed:
		case <-timer.C:
			h.t.Fatalf("screen timeout (%s):\n%s", label, screen)
		}
	}
}
func (h *northstarPTY) expect(label string, after uint64, contains ...string) string {
	h.t.Helper()
	return h.waitScreen(label, after, func(screen string) bool {
		flat := strings.NewReplacer("\r", "", "\n", "").Replace(screen)
		for _, text := range contains {
			if !strings.Contains(flat, text) {
				return false
			}
		}
		return true
	})
}
func (h *northstarPTY) resize(cols, rows int) uint64 {
	h.t.Helper()
	h.mu.Lock()
	rev := h.revision
	h.emu.Resize(cols, rows)
	h.mu.Unlock()
	if err := pty.Setsize(h.master, &pty.Winsize{Cols: uint16(cols), Rows: uint16(rows)}); err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := h.cmd.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGWINCH); err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return rev
}
func (h *northstarPTY) exit(input string) {
	h.t.Helper()
	h.send(input)
	select {
	case err := <-h.done:
		if err != nil {
			h.t.Fatalf("PTY child exit: %v", err)
		}
	case <-time.After(5 * time.Second):
		h.t.Fatal("PTY child exit timeout")
	}
	// Darwin revokes the slave when its session leader exits; the master
	// retains the shared terminal attributes for the restoration assertion.
	after, err := term.GetState(int(h.master.Fd()))
	if err != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(h.before, after) {
		h.t.Fatalf("cooked termios not restored: before=%+v after=%+v err=%v", h.before, after, err)
	}
}

func TestNorthstarPTYSessionNavigator(t *testing.T) {

3:The contract is `/Users/ehrlich/repos/claude/state/drive/wingthing-pairing-spec.md` (11 October 2026). Pair Bryan's iPhone to **EhrlichMac's main wing** first; pair **forge's wing** separately. A phone grant permits session inventory, attachment, input and stop on precisely one wing. It confers no account, configuration, enrollment, shell-launch or other-machine authority. `wt pair` must contact the running wing's local control endpoint, not start a second wing with a new identity.
9:Use **BoringSSL's SPAKE2 implementation**, not a new PAKE implementation. Vendor the symbol-prefixed source shipped by Apple's Swift Crypto at revision `1c80d3aff53fedc445b60692980cb4f19a618657`, derived from BoringSSL `0226f30467f540a3f62ef48d453f93927da199b6`. Go and Swift call the same `SPAKE2_CTX_new`, `SPAKE2_generate_msg` and `SPAKE2_process_msg` library APIs. This is BoringSSL's Ed25519 SPAKE2 dialect; do not claim wire compatibility with arbitrary RFC 9382 implementations. Each context is used once and freed. BoringSSL is a maintained cryptographic library used by Chromium and Swift Crypto; this integration still needs independent review before a public security claim.
11:Primary references: [BoringSSL SPAKE2 API](https://commondatastorage.googleapis.com/chromium-boringssl-docs/curve25519.h.html), [Swift Crypto source](https://github.com/apple/swift-crypto/tree/1c80d3aff53fedc445b60692980cb4f19a618657), [BoringSSL security policy](https://github.com/google/boringssl/blob/main/SECURITY.md). AES-256-GCM, X25519, HKDF-SHA256, HMAC-SHA256 and P-256 signing use Go standard crypto / x/crypto and Apple CryptoKit. No curve arithmetic, password exchange or cipher is implemented here.
13:## Invitation and transport
15:The wing already has a persistent X25519 `wing_key` and WingID. Its SHA-256 fingerprint is the full lowercase hex digest of the decoded 32-byte public key. The QR is `wingthing://pair#v1z.` followed by unpadded base64url of RFC 1951 raw-DEFLATE compression (Go `compress/flate`, Apple Foundation `NSData` zlib API) of a compact JSON array in this exact order: `[v, wing_id, name, key, local, relay, id, secret, expires_unix_seconds]`. Compressing the isolated invitation keeps the real QR within ordinary terminal widths even with tailnet endpoints. This compresses only a locally displayed invitation, never credential/session traffic; the input QR is limited to 4 KiB and decompressed JSON to 4 KiB. The app also accepts uncompressed `v1.` arrays. When there are additional direct endpoints, field `local` in this array is an ordered array `[primary_lan, other_lan, tailnet_ip, magicdns]` instead of one string. The expanded form retains `local` plus optional `endpoints`. The equivalent expanded object below describes the fields; the app also accepts percent-encoded expanded JSON after `wingthing://pair#` for the hidden paste fallback:
25:Local-only is the default and requires no Wingthing account or payment. A personal wing with no valid registration starts locally with plain `wt wing start`; no extra flag, relay lookup, account login or relay transport is required. Even a saved relay URL cannot opt an accountless wing into relay machinery. To keep a registered wing entirely local, use `wt wing start --local-only`. The default setup is:
28:wt wing start   # if the main wing is not already running
38:## PAKE and two-sided confirmation
40:All binary fields below are standard padded base64. Transport requests are JSON objects; errors are stable codes (`expired`, `used`, `cancelled`, `identity_mismatch`, `bad_proof`, `revoked`, `unreachable`, `denied`). Do not reflect secrets in errors or logs.
50:Generate a persistent P-256 signing key per pairing on the phone. Use Secure Enclave when supported, otherwise CryptoKit with a non-synchronizing, `kSecAttrAccessibleWhenUnlockedThisDeviceOnly` Keychain item. Store the opaque device credential and key representation together in a device-only Keychain item scoped by WingID, pinned wing key and device ID. A Secure Enclave representation wraps a hardware-bound key; it is not the private scalar. Public machine names, endpoints, pins and device IDs may be stored in Application Support. Never place a secret or private key in UserDefaults, profile JSON, setup URLs, diagnostics or the clipboard. Each pairing is independent. X25519 connection keys are ephemeral and remain in memory.
52:For each connection, phone creates ephemeral X25519 and derives AES-GCM to the scanned persistent wing key with the existing `wt-phone-v1` HKDF domain (32 zero salt bytes). `device.hello {sender_pub}` returns an encrypted random challenge and random connection ID. The phone signs UTF-8 `wt-device-v1\n<wing_id>\n<connection_id>\n<challenge>\n<sender_pub>` with its stored P-256 key. It sends encrypted `device.auth {device_id, credential, signature, connection_id}`. The wing checks credential hash in constant time, signature, fresh challenge, live device record and exact wing binding. The encrypted hello also returns current direct endpoints and the registered relay origin; after successful device authentication the app saves that public metadata. The existing `wt login` command restarts a running main wing with its new registration; this login-specific restart removes `--local-only` while retaining the same state directory, wing key, roost and session configuration. Ordinary restarts preserve local-only mode. Following `wt login` on a previously local-only Mac, reconnect the app once on the LAN so it learns the relay origin, without re-pairing or logging in on the phone. Challenge is single-use, expires in 30 seconds and authentication failure closes the connection.
70:`wt devices` talks to the same main wing over local authenticated control. It lists paired phone name, paired-at and last seen. `wt devices revoke <name>` resolves exactly one active name; ambiguous names require the device ID. Under the same registry lock that admits commands, persist revocation and cancel all connections/attachments for that device before acknowledging success. New commands fail `revoked`; a relay cannot revive a revoked credential. Re-pairing requires a new QR and both confirmations. On loss, revoke separately on EhrlichMac and forge. Cached content on a lost phone cannot be remotely erased by this protocol.
84:A machine name is a display label, never an authorization selector. The persistent key and WingID identify the wing. A malicious QR can name a different machine “EhrlichMac”; Bryan's trusted terminal and two-sided check are the out-of-band authority. Never claim simulator acceptance proves a real QR camera scan, Keychain/Secure Enclave behavior on Bryan's hardware, or cellular reachability. Those remain required physical-phone checks.
88:The entire protocol above, including PAKE public messages, name/key replies and errors, is carried inside a second X25519/AES-GCM envelope to the scanned wing key. Before local HTTP or relay forwarding, generate a per-wire ephemeral X25519 key and derive with domain `wt-phone-carrier-v1`. Send `phone.box {sender_pub, payload}`; `payload` encrypts the exact request described above. The wing decrypts, processes the request and encrypts its complete response under that carrier key. Thus the relay sees only the routing ID, random ephemeral public key, request-correlation ID and ciphertext; it cannot inspect enrollment state, phone/machine names, PAKE messages, credentials or errors. PAKE authentication and both human confirmations remain mandatory inside this envelope. Changing carrier is allowed only for initial `pair.start`/`device.hello`, never after an uncertain session command.
92:Pairing requires a native CGO build and C++17 compiler because both apps call the same vetted library. On EhrlichMac or forge, build `CGO_ENABLED=1 go build -o wt ./cmd/wt` using Go 1.26.9 and a native C++ compiler (Clang on macOS, `g++` on Linux). The existing `CGO_ENABLED=0` distribution builds retain the previous CLI/wing behavior and explicitly reject phone pairing; they do not replace PAKE with an unsafe implementation. Native iOS SwiftPM compiles the same portable BoringSSL sources. A Linux CGO build remains a separate platform check; no binary was installed or daemon restarted in this task.

    25	func phoneAdmin(ctx context.Context, op, id, name string, result any) error {
    26		if !pake.Available {
    27			return pakeUnavailable()
    28		}
    29		cfg, e := config.Load()
    30		if e != nil {
    31			return e
    32		}
    33		b, e := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(cfg.Dir, "phone-control.json"))
    34		if e != nil {
    35			return errors.New("main wing is not ready for pairing; start it with wt wing start --local-only (or wt wing start if registered)")
    36		}
    37		var control phonepair.Control
    38		if json.Unmarshal(b, &control) != nil || control.Port < 1 || control.Port > 65535 || control.WingID != cfg.WingID || control.Token == "" {
    39			return errors.New("invalid main wing pairing control")
    40		}
    41		body, _ := json.Marshal(map[string]string{"Op": op, "ID": id, "Name": name})
    42		req, e := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, "POST", fmt.Sprintf("http://127.0.0.1:%d/admin", control.Port), bytes.NewReader(body))
    43		if e != nil {
    44			return e
    45		}
    46		req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+control.Token)
    47		client := &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second, Transport: &http.Transport{Proxy: nil}, CheckRedirect: func(*http.Request, []*http.Request) error { return http.ErrUseLastResponse }}
    48		defer client.CloseIdleConnections()
    49		resp, e := client.Do(req)
    50		if e != nil {
    51			return errors.New("main wing pairing endpoint is unavailable; update and restart that wing before pairing")
    52		}
    53		defer resp.Body.Close()
    54		if resp.StatusCode != 200 {
    55			return fmt.Errorf("main wing pairing endpoint refused (%d)", resp.StatusCode)
    56		}
    57		b, e = io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(resp.Body, phonepair.MaxPayload))
    58		if e != nil {
    59			return e
    60		}
    61		var problem struct {
    62			Error string `json:"error"`
    63		}
    64		_ = json.Unmarshal(b, &problem)
    65		if problem.Error != "" {
    66			return errors.New(problem.Error)
    67		}
    68		if result != nil {
    69			return json.Unmarshal(b, result)
    70		}
    71		return nil
    72	}
    73	func pairCmd() *cobra.Command {
    74		var showLink bool
    75		cmd := &cobra.Command{Use: "pair", Short: "Pair a phone with this machine's main wing", Args: cobra.NoArgs, RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    76			return runPairWithLink(cmd.Context(), cmd.InOrStdin(), cmd.OutOrStdout(), showLink)
    77		}}
    78		cmd.Flags().BoolVar(&showLink, "show-link", false, "show the one-time pairing link for the app’s hidden paste fallback")
    79		return cmd
    80	}
    81	func runPair(ctx context.Context, in io.Reader, out io.Writer) error {
    82		return runPairWithLink(ctx, in, out, false)
    83	}
    84	func runPairWithLink(ctx context.Context, in io.Reader, out io.Writer, showLink bool) error {
    85		lines := make(chan string)
    86		readErr := make(chan error, 1)
    87		go func() {
    88			scanner := bufio.NewScanner(in)
    89			for scanner.Scan() {
    90				select {
    91				case lines <- scanner.Text():
    92				case <-ctx.Done():
    93					return
    94				}
    95			}
    96			e := scanner.Err()
    97			if e == nil {
    98				e = io.EOF
    99			}
   100			readErr <- e
   101		}()
   102		for {
   103			var q phonepair.Invitation
   104			if e := phoneAdmin(ctx, "create", "", "", &q); e != nil {
   105				return e
   106			}
   107			err := showInvitation(out, q)
   108			if err != nil {
   109				_ = phoneAdmin(ctx, "cancel", q.ID, "", nil)
   110				return err
   111			}
   112			if showLink {
   113				fmt.Fprintf(out, "\nOne-time pairing link (keep private):\n%s\n", q.Link())
   114			}
   115			ticker := time.NewTicker(time.Second)
   116			state := "waiting"
   117			showed := ""
   118			expired := false
   119			allowed := false
   120			pairErr := func() error {
   121				defer ticker.Stop()
   122				completed := false
   123				defer func() {
   124					if !completed {
   125						_ = phoneAdmin(context.WithoutCancel(ctx), "cancel", q.ID, "", nil)
   126					}
   127				}()
   128				for {
   129					select {
   130					case <-ctx.Done():
   131						return ctx.Err()
   132					case e := <-readErr:
   133						return e
   134					case line := <-lines:
   135						if expired || state == "cancelled" || state == "used" {
   136							return nil
   137						}
   138						if state == "confirming" && !allowed && strings.TrimSpace(line) == "" {
   139							if e := phoneAdmin(ctx, "allow", q.ID, "", nil); e != nil {
   140								return e
   141							}
   142							allowed = true
   143							fmt.Fprintf(out, "Allowed on %s's terminal. Waiting for the phone to confirm…\n", q.Name)
   144						}
   145					case <-ticker.C:
   146						remaining := time.Until(q.ExpiresAt)
   147						if remaining <= 0 && !expired {
   148							expired = true
   149							fmt.Fprintln(out, "\nQR expired or already used. Press Enter for a fresh QR.")
   150							continue
   151						}
   152						if expired {
   153							continue
   154						}
   155						var status phonepair.PairStatus
   156						e := phoneAdmin(ctx, "status", q.ID, "", &status)
   157						if e != nil {
   158							expired = true
   159							fmt.Fprintln(out, "\nQR expired or already used. Press Enter for a fresh QR.")
   160							continue
   161						}
   162						state = status.State
   163						switch state {
   164						case "paired":
   165							completed = true
   166							fmt.Fprintf(out, "\nPaired %s with wing %s. Use wt devices to manage access.\n", status.Name, q.Name)
   167							return errPaired
   168						case "confirming":
   169							if showed != status.Emoji {
   170								showed = status.Emoji
   171								fmt.Fprintf(out, "\n%s\n%s wants to pair. Enter to allow.\nOnly allow if all 4 emoji match the phone. Ctrl-C cancels.\n", status.Emoji, status.Name)
   172							}
   173						case "cancelled", "used":
   174							if showed != "cancelled" {
   175								showed = "cancelled"
   176								fmt.Fprintln(out, "\nPairing cancelled or QR already used. Press Enter for a fresh QR.")
   177							}
   178						}
   179						seconds := int(remaining.Seconds())
   180						if state == "waiting" {
   181							fmt.Fprintf(out, "\r%d:%02d · Waiting for scan...    ", seconds/60, seconds%60)
   182						} else {
   183							fmt.Fprintf(out, "\r%d:%02d · %s    ", seconds/60, seconds%60, state)
   184						}
   185					}
   186				}
   187			}()
   188			if errors.Is(pairErr, errPaired) {
   189				return nil
   190			}
   191			if pairErr != nil {
   192				return pairErr
   193			}
   194		}
   195	}
   196	
   197	var errPaired = errors.New("paired")
   198	
   199	func showInvitation(out io.Writer, q phonepair.Invitation) error {
   200		place := "this machine"
   201		if runtime.GOOS == "darwin" {
   202			place = "this Mac"
   203		}
   204		fmt.Fprintf(out, "\nPair a phone with wing %s (%s). The phone will be able to see and control the sessions on this machine.\n\n", q.Name, place)
   205		qr, e := qrcode.New(q.Link(), qrcode.Low)
   206		if e != nil {
   207			return e
   208		}
   209		matrix := qr.Bitmap()
   210		// Two QR rows per terminal row; explicit colors preserve quiet zones in either terminal theme.
   211		for y := 0; y < len(matrix); y += 2 {
   212			fmt.Fprint(out, "\x1b[30;47m")
   213			for x := range matrix[y] {
   214				top := matrix[y][x]
   215				bottom := false
   216				if y+1 < len(matrix) {
   217					bottom = matrix[y+1][x]
   218				}
   219				switch {
   220				case top && bottom:
   221					fmt.Fprint(out, "█")
   222				case top:
   223					fmt.Fprint(out, "▀")
   224				case bottom:
   225					fmt.Fprint(out, "▄")
   226				default:
   227					fmt.Fprint(out, " ")
   228				}
   229			}
   230			fmt.Fprintln(out, "\x1b[0m")
   231		}
   232		if q.Relay == "" {
   233			fmt.Fprintln(out, "Local network only. That's fine; nothing else needed.")
   234		}
   235		for _, endpoint := range q.Endpoints {
   236			fmt.Fprintf(out, "Direct endpoint: %s\n", endpoint)
   237		}
   238		fmt.Fprintf(out, "\n%s\n5:00 · Waiting for scan...\nSame Wi-Fi: no account needed. Away from home: needs %s registered once (`wt login`).\n", q.Secret, place)
   239		return nil
   240	}
   241	func devicesCmd() *cobra.Command {
   242		cmd := &cobra.Command{Use: "devices", Short: "List phones paired with this wing", Args: cobra.NoArgs, RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   243			var devices []phonepair.Device
   244			if e := phoneAdmin(cmd.Context(), "devices", "", "", &devices); e != nil {
   245				return e
   246			}
   247			out := cmd.OutOrStdout()
   248			fmt.Fprintln(out, "NAME\tPAIRED AT\tLAST SEEN\tDEVICE ID")
   249			for _, d := range devices {
   250				fmt.Fprintf(out, "%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n", d.Name, d.PairedAt.Local().Format(time.RFC3339), d.LastSeen.Local().Format(time.RFC3339), d.ID)
   251			}
   252			return nil
   253		}}
   254		cmd.AddCommand(&cobra.Command{Use: "revoke <name-or-device-id>", Short: "Immediately revoke this phone, including live access", Args: cobra.ExactArgs(1), RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   255			if e := phoneAdmin(cmd.Context(), "revoke", "", args[0], nil); e != nil {
   256				if e.Error() == "unknown_device" {
   257					return fmt.Errorf("device %q is not paired with this wing", args[0])
   258				}
   259				return e
   260			}
   261			fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Revoked %s. Live access closed.\n", args[0])
   262			return nil
   263		}})
   264		return cmd
   265	}

     2	
     3	import (
     4		"crypto/rand"
     5		"encoding/hex"
     6		"fmt"
     7		"os"
     8		"path/filepath"
     9		"testing"

    12		"github.com/ehrlich-b/wingthing/internal/egg"

    14	
    15	// ShortStateDir creates disposable clone-local state that fits Darwin's Unix
    16	// socket limit with a full runtime ID. MkdirTemp's decimal suffix is too long
    17	// in some checkout paths. A physical directory also keeps the control socket's
    18	// canonical address local instead of relocating it to the host's /tmp.
    19	func ShortStateDir(t *testing.T, scratch string) string {
    20		t.Helper()
    21		dir, err := shortStateDir(scratch)
    22		if err != nil {
    23			t.Fatal(err)
    24		}
    25		t.Cleanup(func() { _ = os.RemoveAll(dir) })
    26		return dir
    27	}
    28	
    29	func shortStateDir(scratch string) (string, error) {
    30		return shortFixtureDir(scratch, "")
    31	}
    32	
    33	// ScopedHomeDirs keeps both wings' protected state under the provider's HOME
    34	// write-deny root, alongside (not inside) its writable workspaces and TMPDIR.
    35	// Reserve a one-byte state component before choosing the physical HOME name.
    36	func ScopedHomeDirs(t *testing.T, scratch string) (home, localState, remoteState string) {
    37		t.Helper()
    38		home, err := shortFixtureDir(scratch, "l")
    39		if err != nil {
    40			t.Fatal(err)
    41		}
    42		t.Cleanup(func() { _ = os.RemoveAll(home) })
    43		localState, remoteState = filepath.Join(home, "l"), filepath.Join(home, "r")
    44		for _, dir := range []string{localState, remoteState} {
    45			if err := os.Mkdir(dir, 0700); err != nil {
    46				t.Fatal(err)
    47			}
    48		}
    49		return home, localState, remoteState
    50	}
    51	
    52	// ScopedHomePolicy retains the default sandbox and pins the clone-local HOME
    53	// and its ancestors so a provider cannot rename the protected fixture tree.
    54	func ScopedHomePolicy(home string) *egg.EggConfig {
    55		policy := egg.DefaultEggConfig()
    56		policy.Base.Name = "none"
    57		for dir := home; dir != filepath.Dir(dir); dir = filepath.Dir(dir) {
    58			policy.FS = append(policy.FS, "deny-write:"+dir)
    59		}
    60		return policy
    61	}
    62	
    63	func shortFixtureDir(scratch, stateChild string) (string, error) {
    64		// Reserve the actual routing ID width and the egg endpoint suffix. Keep
    65		// Linux fixtures within the same limit so CI exercises the Mac layout.
    66		nameBytes := 103 - len(filepath.Join(scratch, stateChild, "eggs", cmdutil.NewRuntimeID(), "egg.sock")) - 1
    67		if nameBytes < 1 {
    68			return "", fmt.Errorf("fixture checkout path %q leaves no bytes for clone-local state; use a shorter checkout path", scratch)
    69		}
    70		if nameBytes > 10 {
    71			nameBytes = 10
    72		}
    73		if err := os.MkdirAll(scratch, 0700); err != nil {
    74			return "", err
    75		}
    76		for range 32 {
    77			var entropy [5]byte
    78			if _, err := rand.Read(entropy[:]); err != nil {
    79				return "", err
    80			}
    81			dir := filepath.Join(scratch, hex.EncodeToString(entropy[:])[:nameBytes])
    82			if err := os.Mkdir(dir, 0700); err == nil {
    83				return dir, nil
    84			} else if !os.IsExist(err) {
    85				return "", err
    86			}
    87		}
    88		return "", fmt.Errorf("fixture state paths occupied under %q", scratch)
    89	}

for name in ['storyboard-before.jsonl','flow5-after.jsonl','storyboard-after.jsonl']:

   210		defer m.mu.Unlock()

   213			return PairStatus{}, e
   214		}

   218		p := m.pending[id]
   219		if p == nil || !m.now().Before(p.q.ExpiresAt) {
   220			return nil, failure("expired")
   221		}
   222		return p, nil

   224	func (m *Manager) Cancel(id string) error {
   225		m.mu.Lock()
   226		defer m.mu.Unlock()
   227		if p := m.pending[id]; p != nil {
   228			if err := m.cancelPending(p); err != nil {
   229				return err
   230			}
   231			p.cipher = nil
   232		}
   233		return nil
   234	}
   235	func (m *Manager) cancelPending(p *pending) error {
   236		if p.device != "" && m.device(p.device) != nil {
   237			if err := m.revokeDevice(p.device); err != nil {
   238				return err
   239			}
   240		}
   241		p.state = "cancelled"
   242		p.q.Secret = ""
   243		p.result = nil
   244		return nil
   245	}
   246	func (m *Manager) Allow(id string) error {
   247		m.mu.Lock()
   248		defer m.mu.Unlock()
   249		p, e := m.invitation(id)
   250		if e != nil {
   251			return e
   252		}
   253		if p.state != "confirming" {
   254			return failure("denied")
   255		}
   256		p.terminal = true
   257		return m.complete(p)
   258	}
   259	func (m *Manager) complete(p *pending) error {
   260		if !p.phone || !p.terminal {
   261			return nil
   262		}
   263		if p.result != nil {
   264			return nil
   265		}
   266		for _, d := range m.devices {
   267			if !d.Revoked && d.Name == p.name {
   268				return failure("name_in_use")
   269			}
   270		}

   282		return nil
   283	}
   284	func (m *Manager) Devices() []Device {
   285		m.mu.Lock()
   286		defer m.mu.Unlock()
   287		out := []Device{}

   293		}

   296	func (m *Manager) Revoke(name string) error {
   297		m.mu.Lock()
   298		defer m.mu.Unlock()
   299		index := -1
   300		for i, d := range m.devices {
   301			if !d.Revoked && (d.Name == name || d.ID == name) {
   302				if index != -1 {
   303					return failure("ambiguous_name")
   304				}
   305				index = i
   306			}
   307		}
   308		if index < 0 {
   309			return failure("unknown_device")
   310		}
   311		return m.revokeDevice(m.devices[index].ID)
   312	}
   313	func (m *Manager) revokeDevice(id string) error {
   314		index := -1
   315		for i := range m.devices {
   316			if m.devices[i].ID == id {
   317				index = i
   318				break
   319			}
   320		}
   321		if index < 0 {
   322			return failure("unknown_device")
   323		}
   324		old := m.devices[index]
   325		m.devices[index].Revoked = true
   326		m.devices[index].CredentialHash = ""
   327		if err := m.save(); err != nil {
   328			m.devices[index] = old
   329			return err
   330		}
   331		for connectionID, c := range m.connections {
   332			if c.device == old.ID {
   333				m.closeConnection(connectionID)
   334			}
   335		}
   336		return nil
   337	}

     2	
     3	import (
     4		"context"

     6		"crypto/subtle"

     9		"github.com/ehrlich-b/wingthing/internal/pake"

    21	
    22	type Control struct {
    23		Port   int    `json:"port"`
    24		Token  string `json:"token"`
    25		WingID string `json:"wing_id"`
    26		Key    string `json:"key"`
    27		Name   string `json:"name"`
    28	}
    29	type Server struct {
    30		Manager     *Manager
    31		http        *http.Server
    32		listener    net.Listener
    33		controlPath string
    34		discovery   *zeroconf.Server
    35		closeOnce   sync.Once
    36	}
    37	
    38	func Listen(ctx context.Context, dir, wingID, name string, relay func() string, private *ecdh.PrivateKey, handler Handler) (*Server, error) {
    39		if !pake.Available {
    40			return nil, nil
    41		}
    42		ln, e := net.Listen("tcp4", "0.0.0.0:0")
    43		if e != nil {
    44			return nil, e
    45		}
    46		port := ln.Addr().(*net.TCPAddr).Port
    47		local := "http://" + net.JoinHostPort(lanAddress(), strconv.Itoa(port))
    48		m, e := New(dir, wingID, name, local, relay, private, handler)
    49		if e != nil {
    50			ln.Close()
    51			return nil, e
    52		}
    53		direct := directEndpoints(port)
    54		m.endpoints = func() []string { return append([]string(nil), direct...) }
    55		control := Control{port, randomID(), wingID, m.key, name}
    56		mux := http.NewServeMux()
    57		mux.HandleFunc("POST /phone", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    58			var req Request
    59			if decode(w, r, &req) != nil {
    60				http.Error(w, "invalid request", 400)
    61				return
    62			}
    63			writeJSON(w, m.Box(r.Context(), req))
    64		})
    65		mux.HandleFunc("POST /admin", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    66			host, _, _ := net.SplitHostPort(r.RemoteAddr)
    67			ip := net.ParseIP(host)
    68			if ip == nil || !ip.IsLoopback() || subtle.ConstantTimeCompare([]byte(r.Header.Get("Authorization")), []byte("Bearer "+control.Token)) != 1 {
    69				http.Error(w, "denied", 403)
    70				return
    71			}
    72			var req struct{ Op, ID, Name string }
    73			if decode(w, r, &req) != nil {
    74				http.Error(w, "invalid request", 400)
    75				return
    76			}
    77			var result any
    78			var e error
    79			switch req.Op {
    80			case "create":
    81				result, e = m.Create()
    82			case "status":
    83				result, e = m.Status(req.ID)
    84			case "allow":
    85				e = m.Allow(req.ID)
    86			case "cancel":
    87				e = m.Cancel(req.ID)
    88			case "devices":
    89				result = m.Devices()
    90			case "revoke":
    91				e = m.Revoke(req.Name)
    92			default:
    93				e = failure("denied")
    94			}
    95			if e != nil {
    96				writeJSON(w, map[string]string{"error": e.Error()})
    97				return
    98			}
    99			if result == nil {
   100				result = map[string]bool{"ok": true}
   101			}
   102			writeJSON(w, result)
   103		})
   104		s := &Server{Manager: m, http: &http.Server{Handler: mux, ReadHeaderTimeout: 5 * time.Second, ReadTimeout: 10 * time.Second, WriteTimeout: 30 * time.Second, IdleTimeout: 30 * time.Second, MaxHeaderBytes: 4096}, listener: ln, controlPath: filepath.Join(dir, "phone-control.json")}
   105		if e = saveControl(s.controlPath, encode(control)); e != nil {
   106			ln.Close()
   107			return nil, e
   108		}
   109		s.discovery, e = zeroconf.Register(DiscoveryName(wingID), "_wingthing-phone._tcp", "local.", port, []string{"v=1", "wing_id=" + wingID, "key=" + m.key}, nil)
   110		if e != nil {
   111			log.Printf("wing: Bonjour phone discovery unavailable; scanned IP endpoints still work: %v", e)
   112		}
   113		go func() { _ = s.http.Serve(ln) }()
   114		go func() {
   115			ticker := time.NewTicker(time.Second)
   116			defer ticker.Stop()
   117			for {
   118				select {
   119				case <-ctx.Done():
   120					s.Close()
   121					return
   122				case <-ticker.C:
   123					m.mu.Lock()
   124					m.clean()
   125					m.mu.Unlock()
   126				}
   127			}
   128		}()
   129		return s, nil
   130	}
   131	func (s *Server) Close() {
   132		s.closeOnce.Do(func() {
   133			if s.discovery != nil {
   134				s.discovery.Shutdown()
   135			}
   136			_ = s.http.Close()
   137			s.Manager.Close()
   138			_ = os.Remove(s.controlPath)
   139		})
   140	}
   141	func decode(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, v any) error {
   142		r.Body = http.MaxBytesReader(w, r.Body, MaxPayload+4096)
   143		defer r.Body.Close()
   144		d := json.NewDecoder(r.Body)
   145		d.DisallowUnknownFields()
   146		if e := d.Decode(v); e != nil {
   147			return e
   148		}
   149		var extra any
   150		if d.Decode(&extra) != io.EOF {

package main

import (
	"bufio"
	"context"
	"crypto/ecdh"
	"crypto/rand"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"github.com/ehrlich-b/wingthing/internal/phonepair"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"time"
)

type fixture struct{}

func (fixture) Execute(_ context.Context, _ string, op, _ string, input string) (any, error) {
	switch op {
	case "sessions":
		return []map[string]any{{"id": "fixture-shell", "name": "Disposable shell", "agent": "shell", "status": "idle", "blocked": true, "unread": true}}, nil
	case "read":
		return map[string]any{"output": "fixture output\r\n", "ended": false}, nil
	case "input":
		return map[string]any{"ok": input == "hello\r"}, nil
	default:
		return map[string]bool{"ok": true}, nil
	}
}
func (fixture) Close(string) {}
func main() {
	if len(os.Args) == 2 && os.Args[1] == "--client" {
		runClient()
		return
	}
	dir, e := os.MkdirTemp("", "wingthing-pair-test-*")
	if e != nil {
		panic(e)
	}
	defer os.RemoveAll(dir)
	private, _ := ecdh.X25519().GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	ln, e := net.Listen("tcp4", "127.0.0.1:0")
	if e != nil {
		panic(e)
	}
	manager, e := phonepair.New(dir, "fixture-main-wing", "EhrlichMac", "http://"+ln.Addr().String(), nil, private, fixture{})
	if e != nil {
		panic(e)
	}
	defer manager.Close()
	q, e := manager.Create()
	if e != nil {
		panic(e)
	}
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	mux.HandleFunc("POST /phone", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		r.Body = http.MaxBytesReader(w, r.Body, phonepair.MaxPayload+4096)
		var req phonepair.Request
		if json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req) != nil {
			http.Error(w, "bad request", 400)
			return
		}
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
		_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(manager.Box(r.Context(), req))
	})
	server := &http.Server{Handler: mux, ReadHeaderTimeout: 5 * time.Second}
	go server.Serve(ln)
	defer server.Close()
	_ = json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(map[string]string{"link": q.Link()})
	scanner := bufio.NewScanner(os.Stdin)
	for scanner.Scan() {
		var err error
		switch scanner.Text() {
		case "allow":
			err = manager.Allow(q.ID)
		case "revoke":
			err = manager.Revoke("Bryan's iPhone")
		case "stop":
			return
		default:
			err = fmt.Errorf("unknown fixture control")
		}
		if err != nil {
			_ = json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(map[string]string{"error": err.Error()})
		} else {
			_ = json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(map[string]bool{"ok": true})
		}
	}
}

     1	package phonepair
     2	
     3	import (
     4		"context"
     5		"crypto/sha256"
     6		"encoding/hex"
     7		"encoding/json"
     8		"net"
     9		"os/exec"
    10		"strconv"
    11		"strings"

    13	)
    14	
    15	// DiscoveryName identifies one wing, not a display name or another machine.
    16	func DiscoveryName(wingID string) string {
    17		hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(wingID))
    18		return "wt-" + hex.EncodeToString(hash[:8])
    19	}
    20	func directEndpoints(port int) []string {
    21		addrs, _ := net.InterfaceAddrs()
    22		var hosts []string
    23		for _, a := range addrs {
    24			ip, _, e := net.ParseCIDR(a.String())
    25			if e == nil && ip.To4() != nil && (ip.IsPrivate() || ip.IsLinkLocalUnicast()) && !ip.IsLoopback() {
    26				hosts = append(hosts, ip.String())
    27			}
    28		}
    29		if len(hosts) > 3 {
    30			hosts = hosts[:3]
    31		}
    32		ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 2*time.Second)
    33		defer cancel()
    34		binary, e := exec.LookPath("tailscale")
    35		if e != nil {
    36			binary = "/Applications/Tailscale.app/Contents/MacOS/Tailscale"
    37		}
    38		data, e := exec.CommandContext(ctx, binary, "status", "--json").Output()
    39		if e != nil || len(data) > 1<<20 {
    40			data = nil
    41		}
    42		return directEndpointURLs(port, hosts, data)
    43	}
    44	
    45	func directEndpointURLs(port int, lan []string, tailnetStatus []byte) []string {
    46		hosts := append([]string(nil), lan...)
    47		if len(hosts) == 0 {
    48			hosts = append(hosts, "127.0.0.1")
    49		}
    50		hosts = append(hosts, tailnetHosts(tailnetStatus)...)
    51		var endpoints []string
    52		seen := map[string]bool{}
    53		for _, host := range hosts {
    54			if !seen[host] {
    55				seen[host] = true
    56				endpoints = append(endpoints, "http://"+net.JoinHostPort(host, strconv.Itoa(port)))
    57			}
    58		}
    59		return endpoints
    60	}
    61	func tailnetHosts(data []byte) []string {
    62		var status struct {
    63			BackendState string
    64			Self         struct {
    65				TailscaleIPs []string
    66				DNSName      string
    67			}
    68		}
    69		if json.Unmarshal(data, &status) != nil || status.BackendState != "Running" {
    70			return nil
    71		}
    72		var hosts []string
    73		for _, addr := range status.Self.TailscaleIPs {
    74			ip := net.ParseIP(addr)
    75			if ip != nil && ip.To4() != nil && tailnetIP(ip) {
    76				hosts = append(hosts, ip.String())
    77				break
    78			}
    79		}
    80		name := strings.TrimSuffix(strings.ToLower(status.Self.DNSName), ".")
    81		if len(hosts) > 0 && len(name) <= 253 && strings.HasSuffix(name, ".ts.net") && !strings.ContainsAny(name, " /:@?#\\") {
    82			hosts = append(hosts, name)
    83		}
    84		return hosts
    85	}
    86	func tailnetIP(ip net.IP) bool {
    87		v4 := ip.To4()
    88		return v4 != nil && v4[0] == 100 && v4[1] >= 64 && v4[1] <= 127
    89	}

   356	// daemonPidMatches confirms the command shape emitted by wingStartCmd or
   357	// roostStartCmd. Failure to inspect argv fails closed: a status check may call
   358	// a daemon stopped, but stop/update will never signal an unconfirmed process.
   359	func inspectDaemonPid(pid int, kind DaemonKind) (bool, error) {
   360		argv, err := procinfo.ProcessArgv(pid)
   361		if err != nil {
   362			if errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) || errors.Is(err, syscall.ESRCH) {
   363				return false, nil
   364			}
   365			return false, fmt.Errorf("inspect %s daemon pid %d: %w", kind, pid, err)
   366		}
   367		return daemonArgvMatches(argv, kind), nil
   368	}
   369	
   370	func daemonArgvMatches(argv []string, kind DaemonKind) bool {
   371		if config.Channel() == "preview" {
   372			exe, err := os.Executable()
   373			if err != nil || len(argv) == 0 || wingpolicy.CanonicalPolicyPath(argv[0]) != wingpolicy.CanonicalPolicyPath(exe) {
   374				return false
   375			}
   376		}
   377		if len(argv) < 4 || argv[2] != "start" {
   378			return false
   379		}
   380		switch kind {
   381		case WingDaemon:
   382			if argv[1] != "wing" && argv[1] != "daemon" {
   383				return false
   384			}
   385		case RoostDaemon:
   386			if argv[1] != "roost" {
   387				return false
   388			}
   389		default:
   390			return false
   391		}
   392		for _, arg := range argv[3:] {
   393			if arg == "--foreground" {
   394				return true
   395			}
   396		}
   397		return false
   398	}
   399	
   400	func ParseSavedDaemonArgs(data []byte, kind DaemonKind) ([]string, error) {
   401		trimmed := strings.TrimSpace(string(data))
   402		if trimmed == "" {
   403			return nil, fmt.Errorf("empty daemon args")
   404		}
   405		args := strings.Split(trimmed, "\n")
   406		executable := "wt"
   407		if config.Channel() == "preview" {
   408			var err error
   409			executable, err = os.Executable()
   410			if err != nil {
   411				return nil, err
   412			}
   413		}
   414		argv := append([]string{executable}, args...)
   415		if !daemonArgvMatches(argv, kind) {
   416			return nil, fmt.Errorf("saved args do not describe a %s foreground daemon", kind)
   417		}
   418		return args, nil
   419	}
   420	
   421	// readDaemon returns the one live daemon represented by local metadata. A
   422	// healthy installation cannot run a standalone wing and a roost at once. If
   423	// both metadata files identify live daemons, fail closed so lifecycle commands
   424	// do not stop an arbitrary half of the conflicting installation.
   425	func ReadDaemon() (int, DaemonKind, error) {
   426		wingPID, wingErr := ReadPidFrom(WingPidPath(), WingDaemon)
   427		roostPID, roostErr := ReadPidFrom(RoostPidPath(), RoostDaemon)
   428		if wingErr == nil && roostErr == nil {
   429			return 0, "", fmt.Errorf("both wing (pid %d) and roost (pid %d) daemons are running", wingPID, roostPID)
   430		}
   431		if wingErr == nil {
   432			if !DaemonAbsentError(roostErr) {
   433				return 0, "", roostErr
   434			}
   435			return wingPID, WingDaemon, nil
   436		}
   437		if roostErr == nil {
   438			if !DaemonAbsentError(wingErr) {
   439				return 0, "", wingErr
   440			}

   164	// waitForWingStatus polls wing.status for up to timeout, returning the final state.
   165	// Returns "local", "connected", "auth_failed", "exited", or "" (timeout).
   166	func WaitForWingStatus(pid int, timeout time.Duration) string {
   167		deadline := time.Now().Add(timeout)
   168		for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   169			// Check if daemon died
   170			if !procinfo.OwnedProcessIsAlive(pid) {
   171				return "exited"
   172			}
   173			if s, err := ReadWingStatus(); err == nil {
   174				switch s.State {
   175				case "local", "connected", "auth_failed":
   176					return s.State
   177				}
   178			}
   179			time.Sleep(200 * time.Millisecond)
   180		}
   181		return ""
   182	}

   150	func (m *Manager) save() error {
   151		b := encode(m.devices)
   152		p := filepath.Join(m.dir, "phone-devices.json")
   153		f, e := os.CreateTemp(m.dir, ".phone-devices-*")
   154		if e != nil {
   155			return e
   156		}

   160		}

   162			e = f.Sync()
   163		}

   165		if e == nil {
   166			e = ce
   167		}
   168		if e == nil {
   169			e = os.Rename(f.Name(), p)
   170		}
   171		return e
   172	}
   173	func (m *Manager) clean() {
   174		now := m.now()
   175		for id, p := range m.pending {
   176			if !now.Before(p.q.ExpiresAt) {
   177				delete(m.pending, id)
   178			}
   179		}
   180		for id, c := range m.connections {
   181			if !now.Before(c.deadline) {
   182				m.closeConnection(id)
   183			}
   184		}
   185	}

    20	## Fixture changes
    21	
    22	- Start the actual built CLI with `wt wing start --paths <fixture workspace>`.
    23	  Northstar already supplies a fresh isolated `HOME`, `WINGTHING_DIR`, PATH,
    24	  temporary directory and disposable egg. No registration/account token is
    25	  present, so this uses the ordinary accountless local startup path. Register
    26	  `wt wing stop` cleanup before startup and run it before removing state.
    27	- Use the existing Go `internal/phonepair/testpeer --client` as the phone.
    28	  Drive the real PTY `wt pair --show-link` command, decode its real invitation,
    29	  perform PAKE proof, verify the returned emoji, confirm on the phone, check
    30	  that this alone did not issue a grant, then send terminal Enter. Wait for
    31	  successful CLI exit and drain its actual output before returning it to the
    32	  unchanged card assertions. `--show-link` is the existing explicit fallback
    33	  route; it does not fabricate QR/camera evidence.
    34	- Recover the encrypted grant, authenticate, inventory the one existing
    35	  blocked session, attach/read its marker, and detach. Check that the main
    36	  wing PID is unchanged. The peer keeps its signing key and credential only
    37	  in process memory; the wing persists the actual protocol device record.
    38	- Card 6 now has a genuinely paired device before listing. Card 11's existing
    39	  prerequisite explicitly expects an unpaired-device error. Pair and revoke
    40	  through the real CLI first, verify reconnect is rejected, then run the
    41	  original revoke command against that revoked device. Both calls and the
    42	  real success/error outputs are recorded; no response text is synthesized.
    43	- Keep non-CGO builds compiling through a driver that retains their explicit
    44	  unsupported-pairing behavior. The native Go testpeer path requires CGO as
    45	  specified by the protocol.
    46	
    47	The original card file has exactly two fixture edits: add main-wing startup
    48	and call the interactive phone command driver. Every original assertion,
    49	expected string, command selection, timeout, test name and final fatal check
    50	is byte-identical. The other three storyboard card files are unchanged. The
    51	existing CLI journey assertions are also unchanged; an additional harness
    52	prerequisite test provides a separately reportable executable result.
    53	
    54	## Acceptance boundary
    55	
    56	All 18 original phone cards end with an unconditional
    57	`t.Fatal("REQUIRED-UNRUN: ... physical iPhone evidence required")` even after
    58	all prerequisites succeed. That statement remains unchanged. Therefore raw
    59	card outcomes must remain FAIL, and the physical obligations must be reported
    60	as REQUIRED-UNRUN. A successful Go peer cannot establish native phone UI,
    61	camera scan, device Keychain/Secure Enclave, cellular reachability, permission
    62	flows or background notifications. The protocol explicitly preserves this
    63	boundary. Backend evidence cannot close a physical-card bead.

    65	Results and gate comparison: [evidence/harness/RESULTS.md](evidence/harness/RESULTS.md).
    66	Independent assertion review: [HARNESS-REVIEW.md](HARNESS-REVIEW.md).

codex
I found a readiness race in the new startup helper. It accepts “local control: ready,” but the wing publishes that status before initializing phone pairing. Endpoint discovery can then take up to two seconds, while the harness immediately starts `wt pair` without waiting for the pairing endpoint. That can cause an early fixture failure despite intact assertions. The three completed phone-flow runs in the supplied log did not show this race.

codex
**Assertion integrity is confirmed. One harness reliability concern remains.**

Base reviewed: `71ef5dc50269d2e60f108d967a9162f59956d7fc` (`71ef5dc5`).

Files examined:

- [storyboard_pty_test.go](/work/lanes/wt-phone-harness/wingthing/cmd/wt/storyboard_pty_test.go)
- [phone_storyboard_e2e_test.go](/work/lanes/wt-phone-harness/wingthing/cmd/wt/phone_storyboard_e2e_test.go)
- New, untracked [storyboard_phone_harness_test.go](/work/lanes/wt-phone-harness/wingthing/cmd/wt/storyboard_phone_harness_test.go) and [storyboard_phone_nocgo_test.go](/work/lanes/wt-phone-harness/wingthing/cmd/wt/storyboard_phone_nocgo_test.go)
- [HARNESS-FIX.md](/work/lanes/wt-phone-harness/wingthing/HARNESS-FIX.md)
- Supporting Northstar fixtures, CLI pairing/startup, daemon readiness, phone protocol/server, Go testpeer, and supplied harness evidence.

**No existing storyboard/card assertion was weakened or removed.** Independent byte comparisons established that:

- Removing the added startup call and restoring the single command-helper call makes `storyboard_pty_test.go` byte-identical to base.
- Removing the added helper/test block makes `phone_storyboard_e2e_test.go` byte-identical to base.
- Every existing expected literal, fatal check, command-selection array, test name, timeout and skip condition is consequently byte-identical.
- All 1,841 other existing storyboard/evidence paths compared, including the three other storyboard card test files, are byte-identical.

The execution sequence intentionally changes: the driver uses real `pair --show-link`, supplies approvals, and adds preparatory pairing/revocation. Existing timeout constants survive, but total elapsed-time behavior is not identical.

The new fixture starts the actual CLI main wing with disposable `HOME`, state and workspace; cleanup is registered before startup. The Go testpeer runs in `--client` mode against the real wing, performs PAKE and encrypted requests, and obtains its credential through the protocol. Phone confirmation must leave the grant pending before terminal Enter completes pairing. Inventory, attachment/read, detach and PID checks add requirements without substituting generated output for existing assertions.

Card 6 lists a genuinely paired device. Card 11 first successfully revokes that device and checks reconnect rejection, then repeats the original revoke command. Its genuine second-call error satisfies the unchanged `"device"` / `"not paired"` prerequisite. The existing journey’s live-output revocation checks also remain intact. The non-CGO helper retains unsupported-pairing behavior without adding skips.

**Concern: pairing readiness can race startup.** [The new startup helper](/work/lanes/wt-phone-harness/wingthing/cmd/wt/storyboard_phone_harness_test.go:21) accepts generic local-control readiness. However, [the wing publishes that status](/work/lanes/wt-phone-harness/wingthing/internal/wing/wing.go:1142) before calling `phonepair.Listen`; [endpoint discovery](/work/lanes/wt-phone-harness/wingthing/internal/phonepair/endpoints.go:32) can then take two seconds before `phone-control.json` is written. The immediate pairing command can therefore exit as “main wing is not ready,” producing an early fixture failure. This is a static reliability finding; the supplied runs did not exhibit it. It does not weaken assertions.

The supplied completed phone-flow log records three backend prerequisite/journey **PASS** runs and 54 raw card **FAIL** outcomes: all 18 cards across three runs retained the unconditional `REQUIRED-UNRUN` fatal, with no prerequisite failure diagnostics found. Backend PASS does not establish physical-card acceptance.

This review ran no tests, builds or daemons and edited no files. Evidence results were read, not independently reproduced. At final inspection the full after-gate log remained incomplete and the linked `RESULTS.md` was absent. Physical iPhone UI, camera, Keychain/Secure Enclave, cellular and notification obligations remain unverified.
